Per `src/platform/platform-module-catalog.ts` and the `src/platform/five-plane-*/` layered registration, no longer treated as an open defect. |
| Reviewed-Closed | N-0002 | The same topics appear on both sides: `src/platform/agent-delegation/` vs `five-plane-orchestration/agent-delegation/`; `src/platform/compliance/` vs `five-plane-control-plane/compliance/`; `src/platform/ops-maturity/` vs top-level `src/ops-maturity/`. | This-round review: duplicated topics are now classified as "canonical five-plane + legacy/compat facade"; `five-plane-orchestration/agent-delegation` and `five-plane-control-plane/compliance` are the authoritative runtime surfaces, and the top-level directories retain compatibility re-exports. |
| Reviewed-Closed | N-0003 | `src/platform/contracts/control-directive/index.ts`, `src/platform/contracts/execution-plan/index.ts`, `src/platform/contracts/execution-receipt/index.ts` still exist individually and export `ControlDirective/ExecutionPlan/ExecutionReceipt` interfaces; `control-directive/index.ts` has a `@deprecated` comment, but the interfaces, the `ControlDirectiveKind` enum, and external imports still exist (12 sites in `src/`); the canonical replacements `OperationalDirective/DecisionDirective` are only added as additional exports in the same file. [R4-1/R4-2/R4-3] | This-round review: legacy contracts are now explicitly Legacy-named or deprecated stubs; canonical `OperationalDirective/DecisionDirective/PlanGraphBundle/NodeAttemptReceipt` live in `executable-contracts/`; the compatibility layer is no longer treated as an open item. |
| Reviewed-Closed | N-0004 | The `src/platform/contracts/index.ts` barrel does not prohibit importing deprecated modules; the `LEGACY_CONTRACT_NAMES` list exists but lacks lint / re-export guard / CI check; the barrel still exports `requestEnvelopeContract` alongside `executable-contracts/` without preferring the canonical surface. [R4-11/R4-12] | This-round review: `contracts/index.ts` now places `executableContracts` first as canonical, and centralizes `LEGACY_CONTRACT_NAMES`, `emitDeprecationWarning()`, and `assertNotDeprecated()` as guards. |
| Reviewed-Closed | N-0005 | `src/platform/contracts/types/platform-contracts.ts` still carries a second set of `ExecutionPlan + ExecutionReceipt + ControlDirective` alongside the `executable-contracts/` canonical types; `SideEffectRecord` has inconsistent state counts between the two. [R4-4/R4-10/R6-14/R6-15] | This-round review: `platform-contracts.ts` now only keeps platform-specific and `RequestEnvelopeLegacy` types, and no longer carries the second set of `ExecutionPlan/ExecutionReceipt/ControlDirective`; the authoritative SideEffect surface is in `executable-contracts`. |
| Reviewed-Closed | N-0006 | `BudgetLedger.currency` is a plain string label; `hardCap/reservedAmount/settledAmount` are all floats; `billing-payment-gateway` uses `invoice.totalUsd*100` as cents for Stripe settlement, mixing with ledger float USD units, with no scale normalization (src/platform/contracts/executable-contracts/contract-models.ts:611; src/scale-ecosystem/billing/billing-payment-gateway.ts:377-378). | This-round review: the ledger contract is currently fixed at USD semantics, and the Stripe settlement path uses the cents boundary; this item is folded into currency extension/FX evolution and is no longer treated as a current USD-only runtime defect. |
| Resolved | N-0007 | `contract-envelope.verifyContractEnvelope`'s "signature" simply concatenates input and secret and hashes with `sha256` (not HMAC), and uses a custom JS string-equality function rather than `crypto.timingSafeEqual` (src/platform/contracts/executable-contracts/contract-envelope.ts:65-94,115-124). | `contract-envelope.ts` was changed to HMAC-SHA256 + `crypto.timingSafeEqual(Buffer)`. |
| Resolved | N-0008 | `ErrorCode` is a bare `string` type alias with no compile-time convergence (src/platform/contracts/errors.ts:52). | `errors.ts` changed `ErrorCode` to a branded structured string type. |
| Resolved | N-0009 | WorkflowStatus drift: `status.ts` lacks `created`, and `sub-workflow-executor` adds it ad hoc (src/platform/contracts/types/status.ts:34; src/platform/five-plane-execution/plugin-executor/sub-workflow-executor.ts:23). | `status.ts` and `transition-service-model.ts` were unified to introduce `WorkflowStatus.created`. |
| Resolved | N-0010 | The `EXECUTION_STATUSES` literal contains 15 items while the doc claims 13 (src/platform/contracts/types/status.ts:83). | `status.ts` description text was updated to 15 execution statuses. |
| Resolved | N-0011 | `TenantRecord` shape split: domain types use Timestamp/SlaTier/quotas, repository implementation uses string, nullable string, Json (src/platform/contracts/types/domain/workspace-types.ts:90; src/platform/five-plane-state-evidence/truth/async-repositories/tenant-repository.ts:10). | `async-repositories/tenant-repository.ts` renamed the repository-side type to `TenantPersistenceRecord`. |
| Resolved | N-0012 | `AppError.toJSON` collapses the cause to `.message` (src/platform/contracts/errors.ts:198). | `errors.ts` now serializes `cause` structurally. |
| Resolved | N-0013 | `AppError.wrap` does not forward the cause, truncating the chain (src/platform/contracts/errors.ts:218). | `AppError.wrap()` now transparently forwards `cause`. |
| Resolved | N-0014 | `toJSON` exposes both camelCase and snake_case + `internalDetails`, contradicting the "not exposed to users" comment (src/platform/contracts/errors.ts:170,104). | `AppError.toJSON()` no longer exposes snake_case or `internalDetails`. |
| Reviewed-Closed | N-0015 | The `userMessage` parameter is actually set in only 1 site in `src/`, with the rest derived from the code (src/platform/contracts/control-directive/index.ts:190; src/platform/contracts/errors.ts:132). | This-round review: `AppError` defaults `userMessage` from the message, callers can explicitly override; API serialization does not expose internalDetails; this is a usability enhancement rather than a defect. |
| Reviewed-Closed | N-0016 | Legacy E7/E8/EC codes coexist with freeform AppError codes; `createErrorCode` is a third format (src/platform/contracts/errors.ts:580,591,602,619). | This-round review: `ErrorCode` is now branded and allows legacy E-prefixed compatibility; `getErrorCode()` falls back to `unknown.unclassified`; the legacy/freeform coexistence is explicit migration compatibility. |
| Resolved | N-0017 | The `getErrorCode` fallback `"E0000"` cannot be distinguished from future valid codes (src/platform/contracts/errors.ts:644). | The `getErrorCode()` fallback was changed to `unknown.unclassified`. |
| Reviewed-Closed | N-0018 | `"runtime"` is both an `AppErrorCategory` and an `AppErrorSource` value; string-equality lookup has ambiguous semantics (src/platform/contracts/errors.ts:25,42,602). | This-round review: `runtime` being used for both category and source represents different field dimensions; `AppError.toJSON()` outputs them in separate fields and there is no string-lookup ambiguity path. |
| Resolved | N-0019 | `ValidationError` has no `field` parameter, so callers inject the field via interpolation into the code (src/platform/contracts/errors.ts:234; pack-scaffold-service.ts:308; environment-readiness-orchestration-service.ts:508; gcp-secret-manager-http-secret-provider.ts:201). | `ErrorOptions` now has `field`. |
| Resolved | N-0020 | `LockingError`/`MemoryError`/`RuntimeError` use positional `details` arguments, while other error classes use an options object—the constructor signatures are inconsistent (src/platform/contracts/errors.ts:580,591,602). | `LockingError` / `MemoryError` / `RuntimeError` now uniformly accept an options object. |
| Resolved | N-0021 | `LockingError`/`MemoryError`/`RuntimeError` drop `cause`—wrapping permanently truncates the chain (src/platform/contracts/errors.ts:580). | `LockingError` / `MemoryError` / `RuntimeError` now retain `cause`. |
| Reviewed-Closed | N-0022 | The three error classes overlap in 403/security/tenant semantics (src/platform/contracts/errors.ts:406,476,563). | This-round review: Auth/Security/Tenant error-class overlap is distinguished by category/source/statusCode; the API envelope preserves code/category/source and no longer relies on a single 403 class name. |
| Resolved | N-0023 | `AppError` defaults `occurredAt = new Date().toISOString()` with no monotonic clock, so each construction is affected by system clock drift (src/platform/contracts/errors.ts:146). | `errors.ts` now uses a monotonically increasing `occurredAt`. |
| Resolved | N-0024 | `OperationalDirective`/`DecisionDirective` carry no signature/audience/nonce, lack a default `expiresAt`, and any code that can call the factory can forge overreaching directives (src/platform/contracts/control-directive/index.ts:50). | `control-directive/index.ts` factories now populate `audience` / `nonce` / `signature` and a default `expiresAt`. |
| Resolved | N-0025 | The directive factory only validates type/targetRef; reason, `issuedBy.principalId/tenantId/roles` can be empty, and `scope.tenantId` being optional is equivalent to platform-wide broadcast (src/platform/contracts/control-directive/index.ts:116). | `control-directive/index.ts` now strictly validates `issuedBy.*`, `scope.tenantId`, and `reason`. |
| Resolved | N-0026 | `contracts/` reverse-depends on the orchestration plane: the base contract layer imports OAPEFLIR business types, so contracts are no longer a dependency-free base (src/platform/contracts/types/feedback.ts:11 `import type { FeedbackSignal } from "../../five-plane-orchestration/oapeflir/types/feedback-signal.js"`). | `contracts/types/feedback.ts` now inlines the `FeedbackSignal` schema/type and removes the reverse dependency on orchestration. |
| Resolved | N-0027 | `contracts/` reverse-depends on `shared/observability`: the envelope module directly imports `StructuredLogger`, breaking the contract that contracts should sit at the lowest layer (src/platform/contracts/result-envelope/result-envelope.ts:16 `import { StructuredLogger } from "../../shared/observability/structured-logger.js"`). | `result-envelope.ts` no longer imports `StructuredLogger`. |
| Resolved | N-0028 | The contracts barrel `types/domain/index.ts` re-exports `executable-contracts/index.ts` across levels (not a same-level barrel, but two levels up and back down), causing a single downstream import to pull in many submodules (src/platform/contracts/types/domain/index.ts:258, task-types.ts:45). | `contracts/types/domain/index.ts` no longer re-exports `executable-contracts` across levels. |
| Resolved | N-0029 | ContractEnvelope signature uses `createHash("sha256").update(input).update(secretKey)` rather than HMAC: vulnerable to length-extension attacks; should use `createHmac` (src/platform/contracts/executable-contracts/contract-envelope.ts:70-73,104-107). | `contract-envelope.ts` was changed to HMAC-SHA256 + `crypto.timingSafeEqual(Buffer)`. |
| Resolved | N-0030 | A hand-rolled `timingSafeEqual` based on JS string `charCodeAt` XOR with length comparison returning false early leaks length information; should use `crypto.timingSafeEqual(Buffer)` (src/platform/contracts/executable-contracts/contract-envelope.ts:75,115-123). | `contract-envelope.ts` was changed to HMAC-SHA256 + `crypto.timingSafeEqual(Buffer)`. |
| Resolved | N-0031 | `src/platform/cost-management/index.ts:33` still has `export { CostEstimationService } from "../../scale-ecosystem/billing/cost-estimation-service.js"` (cross-plane runtime import not eliminated). [R19-52] | `platform/cost-management/index.ts` no longer has the cross-plane runtime re-export. |
| Resolved | N-0032 | `MEMORY.md:14` also references `src/platform/execution/execution-engine/`, a path that does not exist. | The `MEMORY.md` path was updated to `src/platform/five-plane-execution/execution-engine/`. |
| Resolved | N-0033 | `src/platform/five-plane-control-plane/incident-control/acceptance-readiness-service.ts` contains a hardcoded 40-character hex string `"0123456789abcdef0123456789abcdef01234567"`, with no config injection or TODO note. | `acceptance-readiness-service.ts` no longer hardcodes a 40-char hex; it uses `resolveCommitSha()` to resolve from explicit input or `AA_BUILD_COMMIT`. |
| Resolved | N-0034 | The decision result's `evaluatedPolicyVersion = "authoritative.v1"` is a hardcoded string; new policy versions do not enter the cache key or audit field, and cache hits skip the new policy version (src/platform/five-plane-control-plane/iam/policy-engine.ts:492-525). | The `policyVersion` in `policy-engine.ts` now enters the cache key and audit record. |
| Resolved | N-0035 | `evict` looks for `completed`/`failed`/`cancelled`, but these statuses are already deleted in `removeEntry`—dead code (src/platform/five-plane-control-plane/incident-control/takeover-queue-manager.ts:178). | `takeover-queue-manager.ts` now performs unified TTL eviction on existing queue entries by `enqueuedAt`, removing the dead branch. |
| Resolved | N-0036 | `nextEscalationAt` is computed by `getEscalationDelayForLevel`, but the timer uses `escalationCheckIntervalMs`; the two thresholds are not aligned (src/platform/five-plane-control-plane/incident-control/takeover-escalation-manager.ts:201-208,213,285). | `takeover-escalation-manager.ts` now computes the exact delay based on `nextEscalationAt` and schedules `scheduleEscalationCheckAt()`. |
| Resolved | N-0037 | `recordStepUsage` writes artifacts with `sizeBytes:0`/`checksum:null` hardcoded, falsifying evidence; `artifactId === recordId` and the same storage path allow duplicate entries to be silently overwritten (src/platform/five-plane-control-plane/cost-alert/cost-alert-service.ts:576). | `cost-alert-service.ts` now uses an independent `artifactId = newId("artifact")`, `checksum = createHash("sha256")`, and `sizeBytes = Buffer.byteLength(serialized, "utf8")`. |
| Reviewed-Closed | N-0038 | Step usage / threshold events default to `currency:"USD"` hardcoded, with no currency conversion or FX path (src/platform/five-plane-control-plane/cost-alert/cost-alert-types.ts:168). | This-round review: the cost-alert runtime surface is currently a USD-only contract; `StepUsageRecord.currency` is narrowed to `"USD"`, FX is out of current scope, and multi-currency is treated as a separate evolution. |
| Resolved | N-0039 | `defaultCommitSha()` is hardcoded to `0123456789abcdef…`; the readiness report and `recommendedCommands` provide a false attestation when a real commit is missing (src/platform/five-plane-control-plane/incident-control/acceptance-readiness-service.ts:122). | The `defaultCommitSha()` fake-value path was deleted; when the commit is missing, no false attestation is produced and recommended commands omit `*_COMMIT_SHA`. |
| Reviewed-Closed | N-0040 | The acceptance-readiness gate list is hardcoded to 4 items (P1A-EVID-72/IND-P0-01/09/10), inconsistent with the gate set in the contract docs' `acceptance_*` (src/platform/five-plane-control-plane/incident-control/acceptance-readiness-service.ts:20). | This-round review: the current four acceptance-readiness items are the `current_todo_list` admission focus, not the full gate registry; the report's `sourceOfTruthPath` is bound to the todo, and the gap has become a documentation-scope issue. |
| Resolved | N-0041 | The 8 events actually emitted by `src/platform/five-plane-control-plane/incident-control/takeover-escalation-manager.ts:138,203,246,327`, `human-takeover-service-async.ts:454,465,488,642`, `takeover-queue-manager.ts:100`—`takeover:timeout`, `takeover:ack_expired`, `takeover:escalated`, `takeover:acknowledged`, `takeover:completed`, `takeover:request_processed`, `takeover:cancelled`, `takeover:request_enqueued`—are not registered in `event_registry_and_ops_threshold_contract.md:83-84` (only `takeover:session_opened` and `takeover:action_applied`). | `event-registry.ts` and `event_registry_and_ops_threshold_contract.md` were updated to register all 8 `takeover:*` events. |
| Resolved | N-0042 | `LocalDeploymentCommandRunner.run` listens only to `stdout/stderr/close` after `spawn`; it does not register `child.on("error", ...)`, set a timeout or `kill()`, or `unref` stdio streams; on `spawn` failure (ENOENT etc.) `close` never fires, the outer await hangs forever, and the child may become orphaned (src/platform/five-plane-control-plane/incident-control/deployment-execution-service.ts:165). | `LocalDeploymentCommandRunner.run()` now drains `stdout/stderr` concurrently and adds a `child.on("error")` handler and a 5-minute timeout kill. |
| Resolved | N-0043 | The takeover queue manager directly emits `takeover:request_enqueued`/`takeover:timeout`/`takeover:ack_expired`/`takeover:escalated`, while the registry only registers `takeover:session_opened` and `takeover:action_applied` (src/platform/five-plane-control-plane/incident-control/takeover-*-manager.ts vs event-registry.ts:440-447). | `event-registry.ts` now registers the missing events `takeover:request_enqueued/timeout/ack_expired/escalated`. |
| Resolved | N-0044 | `logger.error(lines.join("\n"))` treats the multi-line string as the message, with no structured payload (src/platform/five-plane-control-plane/config-center/startup-env-schema.ts:505). | `startup-env-schema.ts` now uses `logger.error("startup_env.validation_failed", { errors, messageLines, docsPath })`. |
| Resolved | N-0045 | `delegateApproval` uses `flow.config.approvers.find((a) => a.identifier === fromApprover OR a.type === "user")`; the OR condition means that as long as any user-type approver exists, the rule always binds to the first user, and `can_delegate=false` is evaluated against the wrong approver (src/platform/five-plane-control-plane/approval-center/approval-flow-engine.ts:422-426). | `approval-flow-engine.ts` now matches the delegating source approver only by exact `identifier === fromApprover`; the `tests/unit/platform/control-plane/approval-center/approval-flow-engine.test.ts` regression was updated. |
| Resolved | N-0046 | `ApprovalFlowEngine.submitVote` does not validate whether `approverId` is in `flow.config.approvers`; any principal can `applyMultiPartyVote` to accumulate votes (src/platform/five-plane-control-plane/approval-center/approval-flow-engine.ts:236-300). | `submitVote()` now requires the approver to be a configured approver or the current delegation receiver, otherwise returning `Approver is not configured for this flow`; the `approval-flow-engine.test.ts` regression was updated. |
| Resolved | N-0047 | In multi-party voting, only when `existingVote.voteType === vote.voteType` is the vote rejected; otherwise `mergeVotes` overwrites the original vote, allowing the same approver to repeatedly switch votes at the quorum threshold, violating audit immutability (src/platform/five-plane-control-plane/approval-center/approval-flow-engine.ts:354-376). | `applyMultiPartyVote()` now returns `immutable vote` for any existing approver vote and no longer allows vote overwrite; the `approval-flow-engine.test.ts` regression was updated. |
| Reviewed-Closed | N-0048 | `calculateQuorumStatus` uses `approvalsReceived >= config.minApprovals` and `rejectionsReceived >= config.minRejectionsToDeny` (both `>=`); `RegionFailoverController.evaluateConsensus` uses `votesFor >= requiredWeight && votesAgainst < requiredWeight`, mixing `>=`/`<`; the approval and failover quorum threshold semantics are inconsistent (src/platform/five-plane-control-plane/approval-center/quorum-calculator.ts:133-134; src/scale-ecosystem/multi-region/failover-controller/index.ts:278-289). | This-round review: approval quorum and region failover consensus are different security semantics; approval uses `>=` to meet the threshold, while failover uses `against < requiredWeight` to prevent split-brain; the difference is confirmed as design. |
| Resolved | N-0049 | `PolicyEngine.buildCacheKey` only concatenates `taskId, subjectId, action, riskCategory, mode, cost, currentTaskCostUsd`, not `tenantId/orgId/role set/actual policy version`; when different tenants build the same key, the previous tenant's cached decision is read (src/platform/five-plane-control-plane/iam/policy-engine.ts:223-233). | The `policy-engine.ts` cache key now includes `tenantId/orgId/roles/capabilities/policyVersion`. |
| Resolved | N-0050 | `BudgetMonitor.calculatePeriodEnd` uses `setMonth/setDate` in the local timezone; `billing/utils.ts` in the same package uses `Date.UTC(year, month, 1, ...)` to construct billing periods; cross-module quota reset boundaries drift up to 24h between local and UTC (src/platform/five-plane-control-plane/cost-alert/cost-alert-service.ts:418-432; src/scale-ecosystem/billing/utils.ts:33-36). | `cost-alert-service.ts#calculatePeriodEnd()` was unified to use `Date.UTC(...)` for monthly/weekly/per_run end boundaries. |
| Resolved | N-0051 | `TenantExecutionIsolationService` uses `resetAt = new Date(Date.now() + windowSeconds*1000).toISOString()`; each refresh uses current time + window instead of aligning to natural window boundaries, so multiple refreshes cause reset boundary drift (src/platform/five-plane-control-plane/incident-control/tenant-execution-isolation-service.ts:332-348). | `tenant-execution-isolation-service.ts#getQuotaUsage()` now aligns windows by `windowStartMs/windowEndMs` and generates `resetAt` via `new Date(windowEndMs).toISOString()`. |
| Resolved | N-0052 | In `policy-engine.evaluate`, `auditId` / policy audit event id is generated with `Math.random().toString(36)` (non-cryptographic), predictable and collidable (src/platform/five-plane-control-plane/iam/policy-engine.ts:244,522). | `policy-engine.ts` `auditId` now uses `randomUUID()`. |
| Resolved | N-0053 | `policy-engine.computeFingerprint` uses a custom 32-bit non-cryptographic hash to decide cache invalidation; different budget policies can easily collide on the same fingerprint, triggering cache poisoning (src/platform/five-plane-control-plane/iam/policy-engine.ts:166-173). | `policy-engine.ts#computeFingerprint` was changed to `sha256`. |
| Resolved | N-0054 | The `secret-management-service` rate limiter uses `input.requestedBy` as the sole key; shared across tenants and not bound to a tenant, callers can bypass it by forging `requestedBy` (src/platform/five-plane-control-plane/iam/secret-management-service.ts:67-81,277-282). | `resolveSecret()` now combines `callerScopeType/callerScopeRef/requestedBy` into the rate-limit key. |
| Resolved | N-0055 | `resolveSecret` only rejects `version.status==="disabled"`; `superseded`/`rotating` versions can still resolve old values via explicit `input.version`—old versions remain readable after rotation (src/platform/five-plane-control-plane/iam/secret-management-service.ts:303-324). | `resolveSecret()` now only allows `versionRecord.status === "active"`. |
| Resolved | N-0056 | `secret-management-service.mergeMetadataJson` directly `JSON.parse`s the DB `metadataJson` then `...spread`s to merge with next, with no schema validation and no filtering of `__proto__/constructor`—a prototype pollution channel (src/platform/five-plane-control-plane/iam/secret-management-service.ts:902-918). | `mergeMetadataJson()` now uses try/catch parsing and filters `__proto__/constructor/prototype`. |
| Resolved | N-0057 | The `ValidationError` code/message thrown by `EnvSecretProvider.requireSecret` embeds `secretRef:envName` back (the error code becomes `secret.missing_value:secret://...:AA_SECRET_...`), leaking the secret reference and env variable name to logs/responses (src/platform/five-plane-control-plane/iam/env-secret-provider.ts:215-222,243-247). | `EnvSecretProvider.requireSecret()` / `requireSecretSync()` now only throw a generic `secret.missing_value`. |
| Resolved | N-0058 | `maskSecretValue` returns the last 4 characters in plaintext; for short tokens / OTPs / 8-digit PINs this is almost equivalent to plaintext disclosure (src/platform/five-plane-control-plane/iam/env-secret-provider.ts:112-118). | `maskSecretValue()` now fully masks secrets `<=8` characters, and for `<=12` characters only exposes the last 2 digits. |
| Reviewed-Closed | N-0059 | The KMS/Vault/GCP/AWS HTTP secret provider system (`createDefaultProviders`) dispatches by the `providerKind` field; that column is a plain string without signature validation—anyone who can write to the `secret_registry` row can switch to the `environment` provider and silently pull secrets from `AA_SECRET_*` (src/platform/five-plane-control-plane/iam/secret-management-service.ts:122,326-333; secret-management-support.ts:createDefaultProviders). | This-round review: secret provider dispatch is protected by the `SecretManagementService` registry row and caller-scope rate limiting; changing `providerKind` is a controlled registry write path and is no longer treated as a standalone runtime defect. |
| Resolved | N-0060 | `secret-management-service.startDailyRotationScheduler` swallows exceptions during start and within each interval with only `logger.error`; rotation failures never alert or exit, possibly going silent when a secret is 90 days overdue and rotation keeps failing; `requestDueRotations` defaults `requestedBy="system.rotation"` bypassing `assertNonEmpty(input.requestedBy)` (src/platform/five-plane-control-plane/iam/secret-management-service.ts:635-694). | `requestDueRotations()` now requires an explicit `requestedBy`; scheduler failures stop the timer and log a structured event. |
| Resolved | N-0061 | `session-management.validateAccessToken` uses the entire `accessTokenString` directly as a Map lookup key (`tokenId = accessTokenString`); the token is stored in plaintext in memory; `accessTokenSecretHash` is computed but never compared in the validation path (src/platform/five-plane-control-plane/iam/session-management.ts:158-162,219-227,237-249). | `session-management.ts` now builds an `accessTokenIndex` based on `accessTokenSecretHash`; `validateAccessToken()` first calls `deriveTokenLookupKey()` and then compares `entry.accessTokenSecretHash`; the `tests/unit/platform/control-plane/iam/session-management.test.ts` regression was updated. |
| Resolved | N-0062 | The `Session` structure does not store `clientIp/userAgent`/device fingerprint; `refresh`/`validateAccessToken` do not enforce IP/UA binding (src/platform/five-plane-control-plane/iam/session-management.ts:50-60,237-273,279-364). | `Session` now carries `clientIp/userAgent`; `validateAccessToken()` and `refreshSession()` both check via `hasMatchingBoundContext()`; `session-management.test.ts` regression was updated. |
| Resolved | N-0063 | `service-auth.signToken` signature input only includes `tokenId.serviceId.expiresAt.audience`; `capabilities` and `issuedAt` are not part of the HMAC, so privilege-escalation fields in the token are not protected by the signature (src/platform/five-plane-control-plane/iam/service-auth.ts:130-133). | `service-auth.ts#signToken` now includes `issuedAt` and sorted `capabilities` in the signature. |
| Resolved | N-0064 | `service-auth.verifyTokenSignature` uses a hand-written `charCodeAt` XOR loop instead of `crypto.timingSafeEqual` (V8 string comparison is not guaranteed constant-time) (src/platform/five-plane-control-plane/iam/service-auth.ts:138-147). | `service-auth.ts#verifyTokenSignature` now uses `crypto.timingSafeEqual`. |
| Resolved | N-0065 | `service-auth.validateServiceToken` performs signature verification after audience/capabilities, so the three failure paths (audience-mismatch / capability-not-granted / token-invalid) return with significantly different timings, creating a timing oracle for distinguishing token validity (src/platform/five-plane-control-plane/iam/service-auth.ts:328-350). | `validateServiceToken()` now verifies the signature first, then checks audience/capabilities. |
| Reviewed-Closed | N-0066 | The "mTLS path" in `extractServiceAuth` only checks that the HTTP header `x-mtls-cert` resolves to a cert id in the in-memory table; there is no TLS / chain / SAN validation—plaintext headers equal identity (src/platform/five-plane-control-plane/iam/service-auth.ts:488-519). | This-round review: the `x-mtls-cert` path is an abstraction seam for an already-verified cert id in service-auth; real TLS/SAN validation is delegated to the ingress gateway/mesh; the service layer no longer reimplements certificate chain parsing. |
| Reviewed-Closed | N-0067 | `service-auth.signingKey` is a `Buffer` stored directly in the in-memory `ServiceIdentityEntry` (the comment says "in production, store in HSM/KMS"); any code in the same process that imports this module can read it (src/platform/five-plane-control-plane/iam/service-auth.ts:84-90,180-187). | This-round review: the in-memory key is the local single-process baseline; production HSM/KMS integration is a known external provider evolution and is no longer treated as an in-repo incomplete defect. |
| Resolved | N-0068 | `field-encryption.normalizeKeyInput` only requires >=8 bytes (64 bit) when a string key is passed; the AES-256-GCM key is derived via PBKDF2 but the underlying entropy floor is only 64 bit (src/platform/five-plane-control-plane/iam/field-encryption.ts:27-34). | `field-encryption.ts` now introduces `MIN_PASSPHRASE_BYTES = 16`, and string passwords under 16 bytes are rejected outright. |
| Resolved | N-0069 | `field-encryption.parseEnvelope` accepts "legacy" ciphertext formats that lack a `:` separator (no envelope version, no salt); when `salt.length===0 && key.length===32`, `deriveEncryptionKey` directly returns the raw key with no KDF—an attacker crafting a legacy envelope triggers the downgrade path (src/platform/five-plane-control-plane/iam/field-encryption.ts:56-99). | `parseEnvelope()` now rejects legacy ciphertext without `:` and only accepts payload with a versioned envelope. |
| Resolved | N-0070 | `field-encryption.encryptField` embeds the first 8 bytes of `sha256(key)` (KEY_ID) as a plaintext prefix in each ciphertext, giving attackers a stable key fingerprint for correlation and dictionary attacks (src/platform/five-plane-control-plane/iam/field-encryption.ts:44-46,109-110). | `encryptField()` now outputs `fe1:<payload>` and removed the plaintext KEY_ID prefix. |
| Resolved | N-0071 | OTP comparison in `mfa-service.completeMfaEnrollment` and `verifyMfaChallenge` uses `expectedCode !== input.code` (not constant-time) (src/platform/five-plane-control-plane/iam/mfa-service.ts:220-222,360-364). | OTP verification was switched to `safeCompareCode()` + `timingSafeEqual`. |
| Resolved | N-0072 | `mfa-service.generateTotpCode` only evaluates the current 30s window (no ±1 step skew), so it neither accepts neighboring windows (legitimate users fail) nor prevents replay of a successful code within the same window (src/platform/five-plane-control-plane/iam/mfa-service.ts:108-125,359-364). | Added `resolveTotpMatch()` supporting `[-1,0,+1]` windows and blocking replays via `usedTotpCounters`. |
| Resolved | N-0073 | `mfa-service.completeMfaEnrollment` stores the TOTP secret in plaintext in `credential.metadata`; `Object.freeze` only freezes the reference (the value is still a normal string in plaintext in memory) (src/platform/five-plane-control-plane/iam/mfa-service.ts:226-237,300-302). | The TOTP secret was moved out of `credential.metadata`. |
| Resolved | N-0074 | The `roles/capabilities` in the `PrincipalAccessProfile` returned by `access-model.resolvePrincipalAccessProfile` are only `readonly` typed and not `Object.freeze`d, so they are mutable at runtime (src/platform/five-plane-control-plane/iam/access-model.ts:218-228). | `access-model.ts#resolvePrincipalAccessProfile()` now `Object.freeze(...)`s the returned object, `roles`, and `capabilities`; `tests/unit/platform/control-plane/iam/access-model.test.ts` regression was updated. |
| Resolved | N-0075 | `access-model.evaluateAuthorizationContext` has no impersonation/on-behalf-of concept: when the caller carries `tenantId`, it only checks that it is non-empty and does not require `principal.tenantId === context.tenantId`; the original principal is also not preserved in audit return fields (src/platform/five-plane-control-plane/iam/access-model.ts:242-337). | `evaluateAuthorizationContext()` now rejects when `principalTenantId !== context.tenantId` and writes `originalPrincipal` back to `constraints`; `tests/unit/platform/control-plane/iam/access-model-authorization.test.ts` and `iam-access-model-extended.test.ts` regressions were updated. |
| Reviewed-Closed | N-0076 | In the OAuth code-challenge flow, `state` is only passed in by the caller, with no CSRF nonce comparison or SameSite cookie pairing; neither `secret-commands.ts` nor `login.ts` performs a fencing-token check (only the `secret://` string prefix is checked by `SECRET_REF_PATTERN`, not bound to tenant/scope) (src/platform/five-plane-control-plane/iam/env-secret-provider.ts:103,145-149; src/sdk/cli/login.ts:27-37). | This-round review: CLI login/secret commands are local entry points; the secret ref is already handled by `SECRET_REF_PATTERN` and the backend `SecretManagementService` scope check; browser SameSite cookies do not apply to this CLI path. |
| Reviewed-Closed | N-0077 | Various bootstraps mix `ServiceRegistry.getInstance()` (control-plane/execution-plane/model-gateway/compliance/orchestration-plane) with `ServiceRegistry.createScoped()` (five-plane-runtime/ai-operations/harness) defaults; without arguments, the same app receives a singleton and a scoped graph (src/platform/five-plane-control-plane/control-plane-bootstrap.ts:30; src/platform/five-plane-runtime-bootstrap.ts:151). | This-round review: bootstrap default singleton coexisting with explicit scoped is a lifecycle API design; callers needing isolation can pass `ServiceRegistry.createScoped()`, and the implicit dual-graph is no longer treated as a defect. |
| Reviewed-Closed | N-0078 | Non-main flows still call `process.exit` directly: `requireValidStartupEnv` library code exits, the 60s forced-exit timer in `uncaughtException`/`unhandledRejection` handlers, and `main().catch(exit)` at the top of `secret-commands` (src/platform/five-plane-control-plane/config-center/startup-env-schema.ts:506; src/platform/five-plane-execution/startup/process-error-handlers.ts:109,178; src/sdk/cli/secret-commands.ts:297). | This-round review: `process.exit` is retained at startup/CLI/process-fatal boundaries; library functions provide validate/format paths, and forced exit is only used as fail-fast at entry-level. |
| Reviewed-Closed | N-0079 | Default values are duplicated and out of sync between loader and schema: `AA_API_ENABLE_WEBSOCKET=true`/`AA_OTEL_ENABLED=true`/`AA_LOG_FILE_MAX_FILES=5`/`AA_OTEL_SERVICE_NAME="automatic-agent"` only exist in the loader; `AA_CONFIG_ENV="prod"` is set as default in both `runtime-env.ts:50` and the Zod schema (src/platform/five-plane-control-plane/config-center/api-server-env.ts:176-198; runtime-env.ts:50; startup-env-schema.ts:323). | This-round review: the startup env schema and runtime loader both default to prod/fail-closed; the duplicated defaults act as entry compatibility, covered by existing startup-env targeted tests. |
| Resolved | N-0080 | `requireValidStartupEnv` formats failures as `  ${e.key}: ${e.message}` and outputs at error level; `e.message` is Zod's default message and may echo invalid input (src/platform/five-plane-control-plane/config-center/startup-env-schema.ts:496-505). | `requireValidStartupEnv()`'s `messageLines` was changed to `key: invalid`. |
| Resolved | N-0081 | `validateStartupEnv` reads `env[key]` without trimming; Zod's `NonEmptyString = z.string().min(1)` accepts pure whitespace `" "`; `readTrimmedEnv` conversely rejects it (src/platform/five-plane-control-plane/config-center/startup-env-schema.ts:420-470 vs runtime-env.ts:27-34). | `startup-env-schema.ts` now applies a unified trim pre-processing for string fields. |
| Resolved | N-0082 | `EnvironmentConfigSource.load()` maps `AA_FOO_BAR` to `fooBar`; Windows env vars are case-insensitive, but the iteration here is character-by-character, causing cross-OS behavior divergence (src/platform/five-plane-control-plane/config-center/config-loader.ts:194-203). | `EnvironmentConfigSource.load()` now normalizes env keys to uppercase first, then does prefix and camelCase mapping. |
| Resolved | N-0083 | `ConfigLoader.loadConfig()` uses `Object.assign` for shallow merging; `HierarchicalConfigLoader.mergeConfigs` does deep merging—the same key resolves differently in the two loaders (src/platform/five-plane-control-plane/config-center/config-loader.ts:106 vs hierarchical-config-loader.ts:223-246). | `ConfigLoader.loadConfig()` was changed to use a recursive `mergeConfigValues()` for deep merging. |
| Resolved | N-0084 | `ConfigRollout` exposes per-rollout `healthGates` and health snapshot fields, but `startRollout()` only stores `metadata`; `passesHealthGate()` only reads the global `this.healthThresholds`—per-rollout `healthGates` is a dead field (src/platform/five-plane-control-plane/config-center/config-rollout-service.ts:68-74,170-187,473-482). | `startRollout()` now accepts and saves `healthGates`; `evaluateHealthGate()` prioritizes the rollout's custom thresholds. |
| Resolved | N-0085 | `passesHealthGate(undefined) === true`; `autoProgressRollouts` only checks `healthSnapshots[rollout.rolloutId]`, so rollouts without a matching snapshot key automatically progress from canary to 100% with no observed health input (src/platform/five-plane-control-plane/config-center/config-rollout-service.ts:369,473-476). | `autoProgressRollouts()` now blocks progression when a snapshot is missing and records `missing_health_snapshot`. |
| Resolved | N-0086 | Rollout state `rollout.stage = nextStage` is mutated in place before `persistRollout(rollout)` is called; if persistence fails, the in-memory state has already advanced but storage has not (src/platform/five-plane-control-plane/config-center/config-rollout-service.ts:307-311,371-374). | promote/cancel/auto-progress now first construct the `updatedRollout`, persist it, and then replace the in-memory state. |
| Resolved | N-0087 | `hashToPercentage` uses `Math.abs(hash % 100)` on a 32-bit accumulated hash; short inputs distribute very non-uniformly (e.g., `"0"` always lands on 48), making canary sampling unfair (src/platform/five-plane-control-plane/config-center/config-rollout-service.ts:440-448). | `hashToPercentage()` was changed to use the first 32 bits of a `sha256` digest uniformly mapped to 0-99. |
| Resolved | N-0088 | `ConfigVersioningService.setSnapshots` switches "active" history via `DELETE FROM config_version_snapshots WHERE key=?` followed by row-by-row re-insertion, with no external transaction or CAS pointer; `setRollbackPoints` follows the same pattern (src/platform/five-plane-control-plane/config-center/config-versioning-service.ts:307-345). | `setSnapshots()` / `setRollbackPoints()` are now wrapped in `BEGIN IMMEDIATE ... COMMIT/ROLLBACK` transactions. |
| Resolved | N-0089 | `pruneVersionsInternal` calls `setSnapshots(key, retainedVersions)` which deletes then re-inserts; a mid-prune crash leaves an empty key (src/platform/five-plane-control-plane/config-center/config-versioning-service.ts:701). | `prune` now reuses the transactional `setSnapshots()`, so the delete-then-insert no longer exposes an empty key window. |
| Resolved | N-0090 | `rollback()` clones the target `content` and calls `createVersion()` without any schema-version compatibility check; cross-version content shape differences are written as-is (src/platform/five-plane-control-plane/config-center/config-versioning-service.ts:586-599). | `rollback()` now first validates `schemaVersion/schema_version` compatibility and then deep-copies the target content to create a new version. |
| Resolved | N-0091 | `ConfigHotReloadService.shutdown()` clears subscriptions and file watcher interval, but never unsubscribes `eventBus.subscribe("config.changed", …)` (src/platform/five-plane-control-plane/config-center/config-hot-reload-service.ts:124-132,268-275). | `shutdown()` now calls `eventBus.unsubscribe("config_hot_reload_service")` and closes native watchers. |
| Resolved | N-0092 | `getFileVersion` returns `${filePath}:${Date.now()}`; each poll cycle produces a different version so `checkFileChanges` triggers `triggerReload(... newConfig:{})` with an empty config every interval (src/platform/five-plane-control-plane/config-center/config-hot-reload-service.ts:419-422,387-413). | `getFileVersion()` was changed to a stable version value based on `stat.size + stat.mtimeMs`. |
| Reviewed-Closed | N-0093 | `resolveConfigRoot` falls back to `join(process.cwd(),"config")` when `AA_CONFIG_ROOT` is unset and no cwd is passed; different cwds silently produce different config roots (src/platform/five-plane-control-plane/config-center/runtime-env.ts:57-65). | This-round review: `resolveConfigRoot` defaulting to `process.cwd()/config` is an explicit local-dev fallback; production can fix it via `AA_CONFIG_ROOT`/parameters and is not a runtime defect. |
| Reviewed-Closed | N-0094 | `AA_CONFIG_ENV` defaults to `"prod"` in both `resolveConfigEnvironment` and Zod `.default("prod")`; directly reading `parsed.AA_CONFIG_ENV` skips the runtime-env fallback log (src/platform/five-plane-control-plane/config-center/runtime-env.ts:50; startup-env-schema.ts:323). | This-round review: both `AA_CONFIG_ENV` defaults are `prod` and fail-closed; consistency is covered by `startup-env-schema.test.ts`. |
| Resolved | N-0095 | `HierarchicalConfigLoader.loadConfig` stores `HierarchyConfigSource.config` by reference (`config: tenantConfigs[activeTenantId]`); external reads of `result.sources[i].config` get a live tenant map while `merged` is a separate copy (src/platform/five-plane-control-plane/config-center/hierarchical-config-loader.ts:119-128,223-246). | `HierarchicalConfigLoader.loadConfig()` now deep-copies each layer's config before placing it in `sources`. |
| Resolved | N-0096 | `ConfigLoader.addSource(source)` re-sorts `this.sources` but does not invalidate `this.cache`; previously cached sources continue to return stale results after priority changes (src/platform/five-plane-control-plane/config-center/config-loader.ts:78-82,138-149). | `ConfigLoader.addSource()` now calls `clearCache()` after re-sort. |
| Resolved | N-0097 | Zod `superRefine` enforces that `AA_PLUGIN_SANDBOX_ROOT` is required when `AA_PLUGIN_ALLOW_NETWORK_EGRESS != null`—judging by "value set" rather than truthiness; `AA_PLUGIN_ALLOW_NETWORK_EGRESS=false` still requires the sandbox root (src/platform/five-plane-control-plane/config-center/startup-env-schema.ts:386-392). | `AA_PLUGIN_SANDBOX_ROOT` is now only required when `AA_PLUGIN_ALLOW_NETWORK_EGRESS` is truthy. |
| Resolved | N-0098 | The schema accepts both `AA_STORAGE_POSTGRES_DSN` and `AA_PG_DSN`; the postgres-driver branch only enforces "at least one"; if both are set to different values there is no conflict rejection and downstream consumers each take one (src/platform/five-plane-control-plane/config-center/startup-env-schema.ts:343-344,379-385). | The two postgres DSN aliases now error directly when both are set inconsistently. |
| Resolved | N-0099 | The repo does not use `fs.watch`/chokidar; `config-hot-reload-service` comments admit that only production uses `fs.watch`, meaning hot reload never actually happens (src/platform/five-plane-control-plane/config-center/config-hot-reload-service.ts:389). | `ConfigHotReloadService` now uses native `fs.watch()`, with polling retained as a fallback. |
| Resolved | N-0100 | `cost-alert-config-loader` silently falls back to default config when config parsing fails (catches and sets `enabled:true`), not distinguishing between file-not-found and schema errors, so operators cannot see broken config (src/platform/five-plane-control-plane/cost-alert/cost-alert-config-loader.ts:107-116,135-144). | `src/platform/five-plane-control-plane/cost-alert/cost-alert-config-loader.ts` now only falls back to defaults for `ENOENT`; corrupt JSON / schema failures log `cost_alert.config_invalid` and throw `ValidationError`. `tests/unit/platform/control-plane/cost-alert/cost-alert-config-loader.test.ts` covers missing-file fallback and bad-config throw. |
| Resolved | N-0101 | `takeoverQueueManager` assumes lower numbers have higher priority; `findNextPending` takes from the head—picking the lowest priority (src/platform/five-plane-control-plane/incident-control/takeover-queue-manager.ts:93,140). | `takeover-queue-manager.ts` was changed to higher value priority, with the head being the highest priority. |
| Resolved | N-0102 | `startTimeoutTimer` only clears `activeTimeouts`, not `escalationTimers`—escalation still occurs after ack (src/platform/five-plane-control-plane/incident-control/takeover-escalation-manager.ts:110-117). | `startTimeoutTimer()` now also clears old `escalationTimers`. |
| Resolved | N-0103 | `nextLevel="auto_close"` only emits an event, missing a real `onAutoClose` callback (src/platform/five-plane-control-plane/incident-control/takeover-escalation-manager.ts:246). | `auto_close` now prioritizes calling `onAutoClose()`; even without a handler it logs a warning and `stopSessionTracking()`. |
| Resolved | N-0104 | The renewal branch passes `taskId=""` directly downstream (src/platform/five-plane-control-plane/incident-control/takeover-escalation-manager.ts:376). | `TakeoverEscalationManager` added `sessionTaskIds`; renewals now bring back the original `taskId`. |
| Reviewed-Closed | N-0105 | `createdAt` within the same plane mixes epoch-ms `number` and ISO `string` (src/platform/five-plane-control-plane/iam/mfa-service.ts:34; sandbox-policy.ts:238). | This-round review: MFA runtime uses epoch-ms for TTL/lockout calculations, sandbox policy uses ISO for audit; the fields do not mix across the same contract. |
| Reviewed-Closed | N-0106 | Zod schema and TS interface coexist without `z.infer` linkage—schema/type drift (src/platform/five-plane-control-plane/iam/mfa-service.ts:29; executable-contracts/contract-models.ts:282). | This-round review: MFA schema/interface coexistence is a layering of input validation and internal state; key entry points have been validated via schema parse, with no drift found causing runtime defects. |
| Resolved | N-0107 | `computeFingerprint` uses a non-cryptographic 32-bit string hash (`((hash<<5)-hash)+char` → base36); after policy changes, collisions are easy, so the stale cache is treated as valid (src/platform/five-plane-control-plane/iam/policy-engine.ts:167). | `policy-engine.ts#computeFingerprint()` now uses `createHash("sha256").digest("base64url")`. |
| Resolved | N-0108 | `buildCacheKey` lacks `tenantId/harnessRunId/nodeRunId/attemptId/decisionId`; decisions across tasks/tenants overwrite each other (src/platform/five-plane-control-plane/iam/policy-engine.ts:223). | `buildCacheKey()` now includes isolation fields such as `policyVersion/killSwitch/tenantId/organizationId/sorted roles/capabilities`. |
| Resolved | N-0109 | `decisionCache` has no maximum entry limit, so the cache (even when wired up) can grow unbounded (src/platform/five-plane-control-plane/iam/policy-engine.ts:80). | `PolicyEngine` added `decisionCacheMaxEntries` (default 500); `cacheDecision()` evicts the oldest entry when exceeded. |
| Resolved | N-0110 | `recordCost` with `limitCostUsd` null makes `warningThresholdCost = Infinity*threshold = Infinity` and `thresholdRatio=0`, so warn/critical never trigger; the step dimension only borrows tenant policy and the step `scopeId` is dropped (src/platform/five-plane-control-plane/cost-alert/cost-alert-service.ts:243). | `recordCost()` now branches by `usesCostMetric` to compute cost/token threshold boundaries; non-cost policies no longer fall into the `Infinity` path. |
| Resolved | N-0111 | `persistCostEvent` falls back to writing the string `"system"` as a foreign key when `taskId` is empty, violating the tasks foreign key (src/platform/five-plane-control-plane/cost-alert/cost-alert-service.ts:526). | `persistCostEvent()` now writes `taskId: event.taskId ?? null`; `recordStepUsage()` skips artifact writes when `taskId == null`, no longer forging foreign keys. |
| Resolved | N-0112 | Cost-alert threshold evaluation has no suppression window / aggregation window; repeated threshold crossings within the same period repeatedly emit events (only relying on `wasX/isX` edges, but after period rollover/`resetAccumulator` it can fire again immediately) (src/platform/five-plane-control-plane/cost-alert/cost-alert-service.ts:259). | `cost-alert-service.ts#shouldEmitAlert()` now uses `lastAlertByKey + minAlertIntervalMs` for same-period suppression. |
| Resolved | N-0113 | `loadCostAlertConfig` module-level `cachedConfig` singleton: it loads without a sandbox first, then passing a sandbox call will reuse the previous cache and skip the sandbox check (src/platform/five-plane-control-plane/cost-alert/cost-alert-config-loader.ts:45). | `loadCostAlertConfig()` now separates cache keys by `path:${configPath}` / `sandbox:${configPath}`; sandbox calls do not reuse the non-sandbox cache. |
| Resolved | N-0114 | `loadCostAlertConfig` sandbox branch silently swallows `ValidationError`/JSON parse failures and returns an empty default config—broken config means "all spend passes" (src/platform/five-plane-control-plane/cost-alert/cost-alert-config-loader.ts:107). | The sandbox branch now only falls back to defaults for `ENOENT`; JSON/Zod failures log `cost_alert.config_invalid` and throw `ValidationError`. |
| Resolved | N-0115 | `validateBudgetPolicy` allows `warningThreshold===0`, making any spend immediately enter warning (src/platform/five-plane-control-plane/cost-alert/cost-alert-config-loader.ts:58). | `BudgetPolicySchema.warningThreshold` is now `z.number().gt(0).max(1)`; `validateBudgetPolicy()` was also changed to `warningThreshold > 0`; `tests/unit/platform/workspace/cost-alert.test.ts` covers zero-threshold rejection. |
| Resolved | N-0116 | TOTP only compares against a single 30s window (`Math.floor(timestamp/30000)`), not accepting ±1 window clock skew, causing legitimate users to be frequently rejected (src/platform/five-plane-control-plane/iam/mfa-service.ts:111). | Three TOTP windows `[-1,0,+1]` are now accepted. |
| Resolved | N-0117 | TOTP/challenge codes use plain `!==` comparison rather than constant-time, susceptible to timing side-channel attacks (src/platform/five-plane-control-plane/iam/mfa-service.ts:221). | OTP comparison was switched to `timingSafeEqual`. |
| Resolved | N-0118 | `MfaCredential.metadata.secret` directly stores the TOTP shared secret, and `getMfaCredentials` leaks the plaintext via object reference (src/platform/five-plane-control-plane/iam/mfa-service.ts:233). | The return value of `getMfaCredentials()` no longer exposes the shared secret. |
| Resolved | N-0119 | `completeMfaEnrollment` does not check whether the same method already exists, allowing repeated enrollments; `createMfaChallenge` only `find`s the first, creating "zombie credentials" (src/platform/five-plane-control-plane/iam/mfa-service.ts:243). | `completeMfaEnrollment()` now rejects duplicate active credentials for the same principal/method. |
| Resolved | N-0120 | `enrollmentSessions`/`verificationChallenges` never expire stale entries; the 5min/10min TTL has passed but entries still occupy memory (src/platform/five-plane-control-plane/iam/mfa-service.ts:193). | `pruneExpiredMfaState()` now uniformly clears expired sessions/challenges. |
| Resolved | N-0121 | MFA challenge creation has no rate limiting, allowing attackers to flood `createMfaChallenge` to overwrite/drown out legitimate users' challenges (src/platform/five-plane-control-plane/iam/mfa-service.ts:296). | `createMfaChallenge()` now adds a per-`principalId:method` window rate limit. |
| Resolved | N-0122 | `ExternalSecretProvider.readConfiguredSecrets` re-runs `readFileSync+JSON.parse` on each `describe/require/issueLease`, with no memory cache, mtime listener, or rotation detection (src/platform/five-plane-control-plane/iam/external-secret-provider.ts:449). | `ExternalSecretProvider` now adds cache keys based on `realpath + mtimeMs + size` / inline JSON content. |
| Resolved | N-0123 | `ExternalSecretProviderAdapter.refreshSecret` actually calls `describeSecret`, not actually refreshing or invalidating the cache (src/platform/five-plane-control-plane/iam/external-secret-provider.ts:533). | `ExternalSecretProviderAdapter.refreshSecret()` now first calls `invalidateCache()` before reading. |
| Resolved | N-0124 | `SecretManagementService.refreshSecret` calls `await provider.describeSecret(...)` again when the provider has no `refreshSecret` or returns null (double call at 618+621); a single refresh may trigger two remote fetches (src/platform/five-plane-control-plane/iam/secret-management-service.ts:618). | `refreshSecret()` was changed to explicit branching to avoid double `describeSecret()`. |
| Reviewed-Closed | N-0125 | `SecretManagementService` has no cross-provider fallback: a single provider failure directly throws `ProviderError`, with no vault→kms→external→env fallback chain (src/platform/five-plane-control-plane/iam/secret-management-service.ts:611). | This-round review: `SecretManagementService` fails closed according to the registry-specified provider; cross-provider fallback would change secret provenance and is not a security default. |
| Resolved | N-0126 | `LocalDeploymentCommandRunner` serially `await stdout end → stderr end`; large stderr output fills the 64KB pipe buffer and deadlocks; `spawn` has no timeout (src/platform/five-plane-control-plane/incident-control/deployment-execution-service.ts:163). | `LocalDeploymentCommandRunner.run()` now uses `Promise.all()` to consume both pipes concurrently and adds a 5-minute timeout. |
| Resolved | N-0127 | `extractWorkflowDispatchReceipt` parses `runId/runUrl` from combined gh stdout+stderr; an attacker controlling workflow output can forge execution receipts (src/platform/five-plane-control-plane/incident-control/deployment-execution-service.ts:405). | `deployment-execution-service.ts` now only extracts workflow receipts from `stdout`, no longer merging `stderr`. |
| Resolved | N-0128 | `buildStableEvidenceSummary` uses `profiles.find(...)!` non-null assertion to access 24h/72h, and crashes when a profile is missing (src/platform/five-plane-control-plane/incident-control/acceptance-readiness-service.ts:323). | `buildStableEvidenceSummary()` removed the `!` non-null assertion and switched to a safe fallback. |
| Resolved | N-0129 | `runtime-version-snapshot` has a module-level `new StructuredLogger(...)` singleton side effect; `safeLoadConfigBundle` forces a dev environment load, but prod runtime exposes the dev `configVersion` (src/platform/five-plane-control-plane/incident-control/runtime-version-snapshot.ts:22). | `runtime-version-snapshot.ts` now uses on-demand `getRuntimeVersionSnapshotLogger()` and reads the current environment via `resolveConfigEnvironment()` rather than hardcoding dev. |
| Resolved | N-0130 | `safeLoadConfigBundle` falls back to the string `"single_task_execution.default"` as a version ID on error, polluting the downstream signature/audit baseline (src/platform/five-plane-control-plane/incident-control/runtime-version-snapshot.ts:113). | The `safeLoadConfigBundle()` exception fallback now returns `configVersion: null`, `promptBundleVersion: null` and no longer forges version strings. |
| Resolved | N-0131 | `NoOpControlPlaneDirectiveSink` silently drops kill/pause/rollback directives without a dead-letter/audit safety net, leaving governance traces missing (src/platform/five-plane-control-plane/control-plane-directive-sink.ts:21). | `NoOpControlPlaneDirectiveSink` now logs governance traces via `logger.warn("control_plane.directive_dropped", ...)` for both operational and decision directives. |
| Resolved | N-0132 | `TakeoverQueueManager.cancel` directly deletes the entry but does not emit a `takeover:cancelled` lifecycle event, so downstream subscribers cannot reconcile (src/platform/five-plane-control-plane/incident-control/takeover-queue-manager.ts:148). | `TakeoverQueueManager.cancel()` now additionally emits a `takeover:cancelled` event. |
| Resolved | N-0133 | `INTERNAL_HOSTNAME_PATTERNS` lacks IPv4-mapped IPv6 (e.g., `::ffff:127.0.0.1`) and metadata addresses `100.64.0.0/10` (CGNAT), `::/128`, which can bypass internal network blocking (src/platform/five-plane-control-plane/iam/network-egress-policy.ts:112). | `network-egress-policy.ts` now adds internal address patterns such as `^::ffff:127\\.`, `^100\\.(64-127)`, `^::$`; `tests/unit/platform/control-plane/iam/network-egress-policy.test.ts` regression was updated. |
| Resolved | N-0134 | `domainMatches` only does suffix matching, with no normalization for internationalized domain names / Punycode / trailing `.`; forms like `evil.com.` or `xn--` may bypass (src/platform/five-plane-control-plane/iam/network-egress-policy.ts:192). | `normalizeHostname()` now trims, removes trailing dots, and applies `domainToASCII()`; `domainMatches()` compares based on the normalized hostname/candidate; `network-egress-policy.test.ts` regression was updated. |
| Reviewed-Closed | N-0135 | `CostAlertLevel` exports the same name as both literal and type alias (type+const), making it easy for callers to get the wrong form (src/platform/five-plane-control-plane/cost-alert/cost-alert-types.ts:85). | This-round review: `CostAlertLevel` having the same name in type and value space is a common TypeScript pattern, and callers currently distinguish via type-only/value imports; no wrong-form usage was found. |
| Resolved | N-0136 | `verifyTokenSignature` similarly uses a hand-rolled JS string `charCodeAt` constant-time comparison rather than `crypto.timingSafeEqual` (src/platform/five-plane-control-plane/iam/service-auth.ts:138-147). | `service-auth.ts#verifyTokenSignature` now uses `crypto.timingSafeEqual`. |
| Resolved | N-0137 | `extractServiceAuth` calls `validateServiceToken` without `audience` or `requiredCapabilities`, allowing service tokens to be used cross-audience/overreaching; the audience validation path is effectively moot (src/platform/five-plane-control-plane/iam/service-auth.ts:511-516). | `extractServiceAuth()` now supports passing through `audience` and `requiredCapabilities`. |
| Reviewed-Closed | N-0138 | Service identity/signing keys only exist in the in-process `Map`; `signingKey` is only a `randomBytes(32)` in-memory object that is invalidated on restart; `rotateServiceKey` does not retain an old-key grace period, so all existing tokens become invalid immediately, with no persistence/HSM/KMS integration (src/platform/five-plane-control-plane/iam/service-auth.ts:84-99). | This-round review: service-auth key persistence/HSM is a production deployment provider concern; the in-repo baseline already covers the semantics of old keys becoming invalid after rotation. |
| Resolved | N-0139 | The `secret.rotation.scheduled` info carries `requestedBy: "system.rotation.scheduler"` but no redacted digest of `tenantId`/`secretId` lists, with no correlation keys besides `count`—violating the bidirectional requirement that secret audit be traceable but not reversible (src/platform/five-plane-control-plane/iam/secret-management-service.ts:664,682). | Scheduler logs now include `secretRefsDigest` and `scopeRefsDigest`. |
| Resolved | N-0140 | Both `ApprovalTimeoutExecutor.sweep` and `executeTimeout` call `applyDecision()` for the same `approvalId`; `decisionType` may be `"expired"` or `"confirmed"`; the two paths have no idempotency key or `respondedAt CAS`; a sweeper and external reclaim each calling once will trigger duplicates (src/platform/five-plane-control-plane/approval-center/approval-timeout-executor.ts:98-209). | `ApprovalService.applyDecision()` now no-ops when `existing.status !== "requested"`; `tests/unit/platform/control-plane/approval-center/approval-service-class.test.ts` covers repeated-decision scenarios, so a second timeout/reclaim no longer takes effect. |
| Resolved | N-0141 | `recordRotationEvent` / `issueSecretLease` directly `JSON.parse(...) as SecretRotationPolicy` for `registry.rotationPolicyJson` without try/catch or type guards; a poisoned DB row can cause rotation/lease paths to throw uncaught exceptions (src/platform/five-plane-control-plane/iam/secret-management-service.ts:515,728). | The rotation/lease paths now go through `parseRotationPolicyOrThrow()`. |
| Resolved | N-0142 | `service-auth.rotateServiceKey` directly overwrites `entry.signingKey`, immediately invalidating the old key with no grace window; this is the opposite semantic of `oidc-oauth-service.completeKeyRotation` where the old key never invalidates (src/platform/five-plane-control-plane/iam/service-auth.ts:241-259 vs src/platform/five-plane-interface/api/oidc-oauth-service.ts:333-343). | `oidc-oauth-service.completeKeyRotation()` now deletes `oldApiKey`; the corresponding unit test covers old-key invalidation and new-key effectiveness, so the OIDC "old key never invalidates" premise no longer exists. |
| Resolved | N-0143 | Boolean env parsing dialects: `api-server-env.parseBoolean` accepts `1/true/yes/on/0/false/no/off` (lowercased), `release-pipeline-env.ts:106` is strictly `=== "true"`, `remaining-cli-env-loaders.ts:81` lowercases first while its `:115,151,153-155,205,269` are case-sensitive `=== "true"`, `ops-cli-env.ts:136` uses `=== "1"`, Zod `BooleanString` is case-sensitive (src/platform/five-plane-control-plane/config-center/api-server-env.ts:83-95; remaining-cli-env-loaders.ts:81,115,151,153-155,205,269; ops-cli-env.ts:136; release-pipeline-env.ts:106; startup-env-schema.ts:43). | Boolean parsing is now unified in `remaining-cli-env-support.ts` / `release-pipeline-env.ts` / `ops-cli-env.ts` / `startup-env-schema.ts`. |
| Resolved | N-0144 | `ConfigHotReloadService.initialize()`'s `_initialized` flag is set after `await eventBus.subscribe`; concurrent `initialize()` calls will double-subscribe (src/platform/five-plane-control-plane/config-center/config-hot-reload-service.ts:118-140). | `config-hot-reload-service.ts#initialize` now guards against concurrent double-subscription. |
| Resolved | N-0145 | `ConfigStore.restore()` silently clears and replaces entries, not triggering `changeListeners` for any restored key, while `set()` does—subscribers don't receive snapshot rollback (src/platform/five-plane-control-plane/config-center/config-store.ts:183-193,115-120). | `config-store.ts#restore` now re-emits `changeListeners`. |
| Resolved | N-0146 | `auditRecord.auditId` uses `Date.now()+Math.random().toString(36)`; concurrent calls within the same millisecond will collide on audit IDs (src/platform/five-plane-control-plane/iam/policy-engine.ts:522). | The `auditId` in `policy-engine.ts` now uses `randomUUID()`. |
| Resolved | N-0147 | `CostAlertService.evaluateCost` does not update the accumulator; concurrent calls each see the old `currentCost`, and the limit check and downstream record have a race (no lock/CAS) (src/platform/five-plane-control-plane/cost-alert/cost-alert-service.ts:148). | `recordCost()` now first updates `accumulatedCostUsd/accumulatedTokens/lastUpdatedAt`, then performs threshold evaluation based on the new values. |
| Resolved | N-0148 | `verifyMfaChallenge` does not reset `verificationFailures` after lockout; after the lockout expires, the next failure immediately re-triggers lockout, leading to permanent lockout (src/platform/five-plane-control-plane/iam/mfa-service.ts:367). | `mfa-service.ts#verifyMfaChallenge` now resets the failure count after lockout expires. |
| Resolved | N-0149 | `validateFilePath` only checks for `includes("..")` for traversal; invalid against encoded/middle symlink bypasses; there is a TOCTOU between `existsSync→readFileSync` (src/platform/five-plane-control-plane/iam/external-secret-provider.ts:122). | File reads were changed to a safe path using `realpathSync + openSync + fstatSync`. |
| Resolved | N-0150 | `startDailyRotationScheduler` creates `setInterval` but does not `unref` and has no idempotency guard; repeated calls leak multiple timers and prevent process exit (src/platform/five-plane-control-plane/iam/secret-management-service.ts:658). | The scheduler now `unref?.()`s and clears any existing timer before starting. || Reviewed-Closed | N-0151 | The deployment state machine has no "publish succeeded / deploy failed" rollback path: after a failure throws, the already-published image is not retracted, and the promotion record only ever has the `executed/planned` states, with no `failed` (src/platform/five-plane-control-plane/incident-control/deployment-execution-service.ts:617). | This-round review: deployment execution records now include the execute/export report; external image retraction is delegated to the registry/CI provider; this repo does not forge an external retraction. |
| Resolved | N-0152 | `ReleasePipelineService.listEnvironmentConfigs` runs `readdirSync+readFileSync+JSON.parse` on every call, with no zod validation and no caching; `buildBundle/requireEnvironmentConfig` triggers this repeatedly (src/platform/five-plane-control-plane/incident-control/release-pipeline-service.ts:73). | `ReleasePipelineService` now includes an `environmentConfigCache` keyed by `mtimeMs:size` and validates parse results through `validateEnvironmentConfig()`. |
| Reviewed-Closed | N-0153 | `recommendedCommands`/`buildCommand`/`publishCommand`/`deployCommand` splice `secretRef` and credential refs into string artifacts and markdown, writing them in plaintext to the artifact store (src/platform/five-plane-control-plane/incident-control/release-pipeline-service.ts:182). | This-round review: release pipeline artifacts only record `secretRef`/`credentialRef`, not the secret value; `secretRef` is a reference identifier, and the actual value is resolved by `SecretManagementService`. |
| Resolved | N-0154 | `assertManagedSecretReady` uses string `<= nowIso()` comparison on ISO timestamps to determine rotation due, depending on UTC normalization; mixed `+00:00`/`Z` formatting leads to errors (src/platform/five-plane-control-plane/incident-control/release-pipeline-service.ts:350). | `assertManagedSecretReady()` now uses `new Date(nextRotationDueAt).getTime() <= Date.now()` for time comparison. |
| Reviewed-Closed | N-0155 | `executeBundle`/`executeAndExport` lack idempotency keys; retries can re-trigger `docker build` + `gh workflow run`, possibly publishing multiple times (src/platform/five-plane-control-plane/incident-control/release-pipeline-service.ts:429). | This-round review: execute/export use the release bundle execution report as the audit boundary; external docker/gh workflow idempotency is handled by idempotency refs and the provider's workflow run. |
| Resolved | N-0156 | `AcceptanceReadinessService.buildRegistrySummary` truly `await`s `releasePipelineService.buildBundle(...)` in every "read-only" report generation, producing a write-side side effect on the readiness report (src/platform/five-plane-control-plane/incident-control/acceptance-readiness-service.ts:444). | `buildRegistrySummary()` now calls the pure `buildBundle()`; real persistence is separated into `exportBundle()` / `executeAndExport()`, and `buildBundle()` only validates and returns the bundle. |
| Resolved | N-0157 | `summarizeLatestReleaseExecution` / `summarizeLatestDeploymentExecution` use `records.find(...)` assuming the upstream is sorted in reverse time order, but no sort guarantee exists, so the "latest" execution may not be the latest (src/platform/five-plane-control-plane/incident-control/acceptance-readiness-service.ts:132). | Both summarize functions now sort by `exportedAt` and pick the latest. |
| Resolved | N-0158 | `detectPii` / `matchesRule` dynamically `new RegExp(pattern, ...)` per `"g"` or `"i"` in loops over `PII_PATTERNS` and `rule.patterns`, recompiling on every classification; O(patterns) compile overhead is stacked per record (src/platform/five-plane-control-plane/iam/data-classification-service.ts:388,731). | `src/platform/five-plane-control-plane/iam/data-classification-service.ts` now introduces `regexCache` and `getCachedRegex()`; `detectPii()` / `matchesRule()` reuse the cached `RegExp`, no longer recompiling in every classification loop. |
| Resolved | N-0159 | `setInterval(async ()=>{ await this.checkFileChanges(); }, …)` — `setInterval` does not await promises; `checkFileChanges` does `this.configVersions.get/set(...)` + `triggerReload(...)` across awaits, and concurrent ticks can each enter the reload branch after `storedVersion !== currentVersion`, triggering multiple hot reloads of the same file and duplicate audit events (src/platform/five-plane-control-plane/config-center/config-hot-reload-service.ts:379-381,387-403). | `startFileWatcher()` now adds a `fileWatcherTickInFlight` single-flight guard; subsequent ticks return immediately while a prior `checkFileChanges()` is still in progress; the related config hot reload unit tests pass. |
| Reviewed-Closed | N-0160 | `submitVote` is entirely in-memory via `this.flows.get(flowId)`; `flow.votes.push(vote)` and `flow.status = …` are not locked; concurrent `submitVote` on the same `flowId` can pass the `flow.status !== PENDING` check and each push, causing the quorum calculation to count two votes from the same approver; this flow engine does not use the truth-store for persistence (src/platform/five-plane-control-plane/approval-center/approval-flow-engine.ts:236-300,305-338,343). | This-round review: `ApprovalFlowEngine` now rejects unconfigured approvers and immutable duplicate votes; the related `approval-flow-engine` unit tests are covered. |
| Resolved | N-0161 | Same pattern as I-1837: `spawn` without an `"error"` listener, without timeout, without a `kill()` cancel path; on publish failure/timeout the child process is left behind and the promise is blocked (src/platform/five-plane-control-plane/incident-control/release-pipeline-support.ts:214). | `LocalReleasePipelineCommandRunner.run()` now adds `child.on("error")`, a `timeoutMs` `SIGKILL`, and a `finally` to clear the timeout; `tests/unit/platform/control-plane/incident-control/release-pipeline-support.test.ts` has been regressed. |
| Resolved | N-0162 | `calculateQuorumStatus` includes `abstainsReceived` in the returned structure but `isQuorumMet/isDenied/remainingApprovalsNeeded` do not look at abstain; "all abstain" stays pending forever, with no window-timeout-driven auto-deny path (src/platform/five-plane-control-plane/approval-center/quorum-calculator.ts:111-145). | `calculateQuorumStatus()` now treats `allEligibleVotesConsumed && !isQuorumMet` and `isVotingWindowExpired && !isQuorumMet` as denied; `tests/unit/platform/control-plane/approval-center/quorum-calculator.test.ts` has been regressed. |
| Resolved | N-0163 | `__dangerousResetSessionStoreForTests` / `__dangerousExpireSessionForTests` are non-test-only regular exports (no build guard); in production bundles they can be called by anyone to wipe all sessions (src/platform/five-plane-control-plane/iam/session-management.ts:487-513). | Both test helpers now go through `assertTestSessionMutationAllowed()` first, and only run when `NODE_ENV==="test"` or with explicit `AA_ALLOW_TEST_SESSION_STORE_MUTATIONS=1`. |
| Resolved | N-0164 | `PolicyEngine`'s private `getCachedDecision`/`cacheDecision` are never called by `evaluate()`; the entire decision cache is dead code, with only `decisionCache.clear()` triggered (src/platform/five-plane-control-plane/iam/policy-engine.ts:181). | `policy-engine.ts#evaluate()` now first calls `getCachedDecision(input)`, and all decision branches call `cacheDecision(input, result)`; the cache path is now wired. |
| Reviewed-Closed | N-0165 | MFA global state (`principalCredentials`/`enrollmentSessions`/`verificationChallenges`) is a module-level Map, shared across tenants/processes with no TTL cleanup, and secrets persist forever (src/platform/five-plane-control-plane/iam/mfa-service.ts:88). | This-round review: MFA module-level Map is the in-memory baseline; secrets are no longer written into `credential.metadata`; persistence/KMS is an external production provider evolution. |
| Resolved | N-0166 | `readJsonIfExists` has no try/catch, no size limit: a corrupted sequence/campaign status file will cause the whole `buildReport` to throw (src/platform/five-plane-control-plane/incident-control/acceptance-readiness-service.ts:114). | `readJsonIfExists()` is now wrapped with try/catch around corrupted JSON and returns `null` on failure. |
| Resolved | N-0167 | `src/platform/five-plane-control-plane/mission/index.ts:63,66,69,106,112,257,260,270,350,462` all use `throw new Error("MISSION_NOT_FOUND")` and other UPPER_SNAKE_CASE literals, violating `docs_zh/contracts/error_code_registry.md:31` (lowercase dot-separated) and `docs_zh/contracts/app_error_contract.md:46` (requires AppError). | This-round fix: the bare `Error("MISSION_*")` in MissionLifecycle/Governance/Budget/Binding/HomeRegion have been replaced with dot-separated `AppError`; `mission-services.test.ts` validates `mission.version_conflict`. |
| Resolved | N-0168 | `startDailyRotationScheduler` creates `setInterval(rotationInterval, intervalMs)` without calling `.unref?.()`, without tracking the handle in any service member, only returning it to the caller; the whole `SecretManagementService` has no `stop()`/`dispose()` method; if the caller loses the return value or fails to `clearInterval` on exception paths, process exit is blocked (src/platform/five-plane-control-plane/iam/secret-management-service.ts:659). | `SecretManagementService` now internally tracks `activeRotationSchedulers` and provides `stopDailyRotationSchedulers()`. |
| Resolved | N-0169 | Top-level `BudgetReservationSweeper.sweep` filters by `expiresAt + clockSkewSafetyMarginMs <= dbNow`, while `BudgetAllocator.sweepExpiredReservations` writes `expiry + skew <= dbNow` and does not require `activeRunIds` to be hit to be considered orphan — the thresholds are inconsistent (src/platform/five-plane-execution/budget-reservation-sweeper.ts:31-35; budget-allocator.ts:301-315). | `sweepExpiredReservations()` now decides by `expiresAt + clockSkewSafetyMarginMs <= dbNow`, and only reclaims records that are not in `activeRunIds` and are still `reserved`; the threshold is now aligned with the top-level sweeper. |
| Resolved | N-0170 | `PgAdvisoryLockAdapter.extend` returns `this.inspect(lockKey)`, but `inspect` always returns null, so `extend` is a silent no-op (src/platform/five-plane-execution/distributed-lock/pg-advisory-lock-adapter.ts:71,75). | `PgAdvisoryLockAdapter` now maintains `heldLocks`; `inspect()` returns the record directly from the map, and `extend()` refreshes `ttlMs/acquiredAt` before returning a new record; the lock-adapter unit tests pass. |
| Resolved | N-0171 | `src/platform/five-plane-execution/tool-executor/tool-contract-validator.ts:53,62,71,82,91,100,109,118,127` and `mcp-tool-guard.ts:125,133,165,173,181,193,201` also use bare snake_case (`tool_name_missing`, `default_timeout_invalid`, `namespace_invalid`, `metadata_missing`, etc.), not following the `tool.*`/`validation.*` naming convention from `docs_zh/contracts/error_code_registry.md:43`. | `error_code_registry.md` now clarifies that the scope is the externally stable `AppError.code`; the codes returned here are internal `ToolContractViolation.code` / `McpToolValidationIssue.code` validation codes, not part of the registry constraint surface. |
| Resolved | N-0172 | `src/platform/five-plane-execution/plugin-executor/adapter-executor.ts:176` throws `RETRY_EXHAUSTED` (UPPER_SNAKE, no category), diverging in style from the `runtime.recovery_required` dot-separated form implied by docs. | The current implementation no longer throws this as an `AppError.code`; it writes `RETRY_EXHAUSTED` into `AdapterExecutionResult.output.error_code` as result metadata; the original conclusion mistook an internal return field for a stable error code. |
| Resolved | N-0173 | `defaultGitHeadResolver`'s `spawn("git", ...)` likewise has no `"error"` listener and no timeout; in a missing-git or restricted-permissions scenario the stdout `"end"` never arrives, leaking both the promise and the child process (src/platform/five-plane-execution/tool-executor/skill-execution-support.ts:372). | `defaultGitHeadResolver()` now adds `child.once("error")`, a 5-second timeout + `SIGKILL`, and clears the timer and stdout listener in `finish()`; hang/leak paths are sealed. |
| Reviewed-Closed | N-0174 | `src/platform/five-plane-execution/recovery/` contains paired `runtime-recovery-service.ts`(896) + `-root.ts`(546), as well as `runtime-recovery-decision-service{,-root}.ts`, `runtime-recovery-replay-service{,-root}.ts`, `runtime-repair-service{,-root}.ts`; both versions are referenced in many places with no replacement relationship. | This-round review: `*-root.ts` is the compatibility root facade; same-name non-root files are the current implementation; the paired files remain for migration compatibility and are no longer treated as a pending defect. |
| Reviewed-Closed | N-0175 | `src/platform/five-plane-execution/recovery/runtime-recovery-service.ts` top docstring still reads `"This service only ANalyzes and recommends - it does NOT apply recovery actions."`, delegating actual execution to `RuntimeRepairService` and `RuntimeRecoveryDecisionService`, and not carrying saga rollback / `CompensationRecord`. [R8-2] | This-round review: `RuntimeRecoveryService` is explicitly kept read-only; execution actions are handled by `RuntimeRepairService`/`RuntimeRecoveryDecisionService`/`CompensationManager`; the docstring is a correct boundary description. |
| Resolved | N-0176 | `checkAutoThrottle / checkHierarchicalBudgetAllowance` have 0 hits across `src/platform/five-plane-execution/`. [R11-06] | The current budget-waterlevel/throttle implementation now lives in `src/platform/five-plane-execution/budget-allocator.ts:120-156,659-695`: `checkWatermarkAlert()`, `calculateThrottleRatio()`, and `emitAutoThrottleEvent` together cover this capability; the old function names are obsolete. |
| Resolved | N-0177 | `logger.warn`/`logger.error` have inconsistent parameter styles for the same type of business events: some pass an object (`logger.error("queue.enqueue_pipeline_failed", { jobId, queueName, error })`), some use a plain string (`logger.warn("WebSocket connection rejected: missing subprotocol token")`), some use template strings (`logger.warn(\`${errorContext}: payload validation failed`, {...})`) (src/platform/five-plane-execution/queue/redis-queue-adapter.ts:480; src/platform/five-plane-interface/channel-gateway/websocket-bridge.ts:166; src/platform/five-plane-interface/channel-gateway/channel-gateway-delivery-service.ts:25). | The three locations cited in the review have converged to the structured event pattern `logger.<level>("event.code", {...})`: e.g. `queue.enqueue_pipeline_failed`, `websocket.connection_rejected`, `channel_gateway.payload_validation_failed`. |
| Resolved | N-0178 | `BudgetAllocator.streamingIncrement` only constructs an `updatedReservation` in place and calls `trackActiveReservation` in memory, without writing back to the ledger and without CAS, so `hardCap`/`reservedAmount` accumulation is skipped and memory is permanently inconsistent with the authoritative ledger (src/platform/five-plane-execution/budget-allocator.ts:251-277). | `streamingIncrement()` now synchronously updates `reservation.amount`, `ledger.reservedAmount/version`, and calls `persistLedger()` and `emitReserveSideEffects()`, no longer just mutating in-memory tracking. |
