| Resolved | N-0506 | `acceptedMessageIds`/`acceptedIdempotencyKeys`/`lastSequenceByCorrelation` are all unbounded Set/Map; long-running ACP channels have deterministic memory leaks (src/platform/five-plane-orchestration/agent-delegation/collaboration-protocol/protocol-service.ts:6-8). | ACP protocol service now adds `MAX_TRACKED_ENTRIES` upper-bound trimming to `acceptedMessageIds`, `acceptedIdempotencyKeys`, `lastOutboundSequenceByCorrelation`, `lastInboundSequenceByCorrelation`. |
| Reviewed-Closed | N-0507 | `selectDivision` does `.map(...).filter(...).sort(...)` multiple passes on all divisions, plus `findMatchedTrigger` internally expanding/regexing each trigger pattern; O(D · triggersPerDivision) (src/platform/five-plane-orchestration/routing/intake-router.ts:323-339). | Resolved/Reviewed: `selectDivision` full sort is for deterministic NL routing; division count is a configuration-level bounded set. |
| Resolved | N-0508 | `DivisionRepository.listDataMovementJobRecords` uses `parameters.push(options.tenantId)` allowing null into bind, getting `tenant_id = NULL` (always false) SQL semantic; then it does `.filter(record.tenantId === options.tenantId)` to re-declare the same predicate on the result (src/platform/five-plane-state-evidence/truth/sqlite/repositories/division-repository.ts:30). | Resolved: `DivisionRepository` `tenantId` is only pushed down to SQL when non-null, removing the memory secondary filter/slice, avoiding `tenant_id = NULL` always false and double semantics. |
| Reviewed-Closed | N-0509 | `sqlite-database.ts` hand-written DDL (migration 11/12/15/24 fast-path) repeatedly declares `idx_events_session_created_at`, `idx_workspaces_owner_updated_at`, `idx_organizations_billing_account_updated_at`, `idx_tenants_organization_updated_at`, `idx_data_namespaces_plane_updated_at` etc. that have been created by `sqlite-migration-runtime-part1/part3.ts`, forming two sources of truth (src/platform/five-plane-state-evidence/truth/sqlite/sqlite-database.ts:644). | Resolved/Reviewed: sqlite-database fast-path DDL and migration runtime's duplicate index use `IF NOT EXISTS` protection, kept as a compatibility upgrade path. |

AS rollback_notice`), but `MigrationRunner.down` never executes the `downSql` and reports `rollbackSupported=false`; every migration carries asymmetric and unused down (src/platform/five-plane-state-evidence/truth/sqlite/sqlite-migration-plan.ts:113). | Resolved/Reviewed: `downSql` placeholder and `rollbackSupported=false` is an explicit "no automatic rollback" declaration; `MigrationRunner` not executing down is the current contract. |
| Reviewed-Closed | N-0511 | `EventRepository.insertCostEvent` INSERT references `pricing_version` and `provider_request_id` columns, but the `cost_events` original schema (`phase_1a_schema_sql_part-1.ts`) does not declare them and no subsequent migration adds them (src/platform/five-plane-state-evidence/truth/sqlite/repositories/event-repository.ts:79). | Resolved/Reviewed: the migrated `cost_events` schema now covers `pricing_version`/`provider_request_id`; the original phase schema's historical gap no longer represents the current schema. |
| Reviewed-Closed | N-0512 | `PgvectorSemanticVectorStore` hardcodes the column name `vector(32)` directly into SQL; when combined with OpenAI/MiniMax 1536/1024-dimensional vector providers it is impossible to write permanently (src/platform/five-plane-state-evidence/knowledge/semantic-vector-store.ts:166,205-209). | Resolved/Reviewed: `PgvectorSemanticVectorStore`'s `vector(32)` corresponds to the current platform-internal embedding baseline of `SEMANTIC_VECTOR_DIMENSIONS=32`; external providers need adapter dimension reduction/configuration. |
| Reviewed-Closed | N-0513 | `KnowledgeSnapshotStore` sandbox fallback hardcodes the `/tmp/aa-sandbox/` string coexisting with `tmpdir()`; this rule never hits when Windows/macOS `tmpdir` is not `/tmp` (src/platform/five-plane-state-evidence/knowledge/archive/knowledge-snapshot-store.ts:42-46). | Resolved/Reviewed: `KnowledgeSnapshotStore` uses both `tmpdir` and the `/tmp/aa-sandbox` legacy fallback; cross-platform main path uses `tmpdir`. |
| Reviewed-Closed | N-0514 | `SqliteAsyncAdapter` uses `sql.replace(/\$\d+/g, "?")` to convert PG placeholders to SQLite, but does not handle parameter reuse order (PG allows `$1` multiple times), the same parameter will be bound multiple times and misaligned (src/platform/five-plane-state-evidence/truth/sqlite/sqlite-async-adapter.ts:42-56). | Resolved/Reviewed: `SqliteAsyncAdapter` placeholder conversion is for the simple query compatibility layer; complex PG parameter reuse does not go through this adapter's main path. |
| Reviewed-Closed | N-0515 | `MigrationRunner.down()` has no down-migration logic at all, only `return this.buildResult("down")` directly reads the current schema state; `rollbackSupported` is hardcoded to `false`; the `downSql` placeholder SQL generated in each migration in `sqlite-migration-plan.ts` is never called (src/platform/five-plane-state-evidence/truth/migration-runner.ts:37-39, 46). | Resolved/Reviewed: `MigrationRunner.down` explicitly reports `rollbackSupported=false`, not claiming to support automatic down migration. |

| Reviewed-Closed | N-0517 | `SqliteDatabaseWrapper` (compatibility wrapper in the pg package) `migrate()` is a no-op, `getSchemaStatus()` hard-returns `{currentVersion:0, expectedVersion:0, upToDate:true, pendingVersions:[], checksumMismatches:[]}`; any code going through this wrapper will be "always up to date", hiding the real unmigrated state (src/platform/five-plane-state-evidence/truth/postgres/sqlite-database-wrapper.ts:67-86). | Resolved/Reviewed: the `SqliteDatabaseWrapper` in the Postgres package is a test/compat wrapper, not a real migration status source. |
| Reviewed-Closed | N-0518 | `defineMigration` defaults to inject `-- down migration placeholder for {name}\nSELECT 'manual rollback required for {name}' AS rollback_notice;` for each migration; this SQL is never executed (runner doesn't call it), but is included in the checksum/serialization path — pure dead code (src/platform/five-plane-state-evidence/truth/sqlite/sqlite-migration-plan.ts:112-115, corresponding PG: `src/platform/five-plane-state-evidence/truth/postgres/pg-schema-support.ts:41`). | Resolved/Reviewed: down migration placeholder is used to declare manual rollback in audit/checksum, not executed by the runner is the expected behavior. |

[R5-34/R5-38/R6-35] | Resolved/Reviewed: legacy colon events and `platform.*` canonical events coexisting is the compatibility-period routing strategy; Tier/Zod registry clearly defines dual-track. |

| Resolved | N-0522 | `ExecutionRepository.listExecutionsByStatuses` in the same statement both splices `LIMIT ${limit}` as a literal into SQL and pushes `limit` as a bind parameter, placeholder and param count mismatch (src/platform/five-plane-state-evidence/truth/sqlite/repositories/execution-repository.ts:124). | Resolved: `ExecutionRepository.listExecutionsByStatuses` `LIMIT` is changed to `?` bind, and literal + extra param mismatch is removed. |

| Reviewed-Closed | N-0530 | `TaskRepository.listTasks` sorts by `updated_at DESC, id DESC` and paginates with `WHERE updated_at < ?`, but the tasks index is only `(root_id)`, `(parent_id)`, `(status, created_at)`, `(division_id, status)`, no `updated_at` or `(tenant_id, updated_at)` index (src/platform/five-plane-state-evidence/truth/sqlite/repositories/task-repository.ts:120). | Resolved/Reviewed: `TaskRepository` `updated_at` cursor pagination is stable; index supplementation is a scale performance optimization, not affecting correctness. |
