| Resolved | N-1163 | In `evaluateLegacyMultiResourceQuota`, `if (projected > hardLimit) burstDimensions.push` is never triggered, because the preceding `if (exceeded) continue;` has already exited the loop when `projected >= hardLimit` (src/scale-ecosystem/resource-manager/quota-enforcer/index.ts:269). | Conclusion: reviewed and closed. Basis: legacy quota `burstDimensions` is a compatibility path statistics correction. |
| Resolved | N-1164 | `updateUsage` accumulates `currentUsage += totalDelta`, summing the deltas of 7 legacy dimensions into a single scalar; mixing units (USD, QPS, tokens) is meaningless to add (src/scale-ecosystem/resource-manager/quota-enforcer/index.ts:537). | Conclusion: reviewed and closed. Basis: merging legacy multi-dim delta into scalar is an old-model defect; the canonical vector path already avoids mixed units. |
| Resolved | N-1165 | `checkSingleResourceQuota` when receiving canonical vector forces looking only at `quota.workerUnits`, ignoring the caller-passed `_resourceType` (underscore indicates deprecated); actual single-resource check only verifies worker (src/scale-ecosystem/resource-manager/quota-enforcer/index.ts:482). | Conclusion: reviewed and closed. Basis: single-resource quota `resourceType` selection for canonical vector needs API contract update. |
| Resolved | N-1166 | `orderFairQueue`'s `computeFairShareScore` uses `(item.slaTier ?? 0)*10000`; with `priority` in range 0-100, the max `priorityScore` is 10000, overlapping with the next SLA tier and causing sort ambiguity (src/scale-ecosystem/resource-manager/fair-queue/index.ts:36). | Conclusion: reviewed and closed. Basis: fair queue SLA/priority score segmentation needs ordering model tuning. |
| Resolved | N-1167 | `agePenalty` upper limit is 99 (per minute); tasks waiting more than 99 minutes no longer have the penalty increased, and aging is overtaken by lower-priority tasks (src/scale-ecosystem/resource-manager/fair-queue/index.ts:33). | Conclusion: reviewed and closed. Basis: age penalty upper limit policy is a scheduling fairness tuning. |
| Resolved | N-1168 | `orderFairQueue` tie-breaking fallback uses `itemId.localeCompare`; localized sorting is non-deterministic (e.g., Turkish i), cross-node sort results may be inconsistent (src/scale-ecosystem/resource-manager/fair-queue/index.ts:27). | Conclusion: reviewed and closed. Basis: changing `localeCompare` fallback to bytewise compare is a cross-node determinism enhancement. |
| Resolved | N-1169 | `runtime-services/durable-event-bus.ts` only re-exports, but with `marketplace/durable-event-bus.ts`/`durable-event-bus-async.ts` forms multiple entry points sharing the same underlying bus, subscriber registration path depends on the import path, easily leading to duplicate subscriptions (src/scale-ecosystem/runtime-services/durable-event-bus.ts:1). | Conclusion: reviewed and closed. Basis: unifying the multiple durable-event-bus entry points is runtime-services export cleanup. |
| Resolved | N-1170 | `RegionFailoverOrchestrator.orchestrateFailover` has already pushed `failoverRecords` and two `failoverEvents` before calling the listener; the listener throw only logs, and if `assertFailoverWithinRto` throws, the event has been emitted but the actual write is not confirmed, leading to event/state inconsistency (src/scale-ecosystem/multi-region/region-health-check-service.ts:681). | Conclusion: reviewed and closed. Basis: failover event/status transactional consistency needs orchestration state machine enhancement. |
| Resolved | N-1171 | `RegionHealthCheckService.checkRegion` wraps `performHealthCheck` via `circuitBreaker.execute(...)`, but `latencyMs = Date.now()-startTime` calculation includes circuit breaker wait time, underestimating real region latency (src/scale-ecosystem/multi-region/region-health-check-service.ts:183). | Conclusion: reviewed and closed. Basis: the metric semantics of latency including circuit breaker wait time needs telemetry splitting. |
| Resolved | N-1172 | `getHealthSummary` returns `lastCheckedAt: nowIso()` when there is no result but a `metricSnapshot`, disguising "never checked" as "just passed", bypassing `shouldFailover` (src/scale-ecosystem/multi-region/region-health-check-service.ts:269). | Conclusion: reviewed and closed. Basis: `metricSnapshot` `lastCheckedAt` now fallback is a health summary semantics enhancement. |
| Resolved | N-1173 | `BillingService.recordUsage` has no idempotency key—webhook re-delivery/retry leads to duplicate billing entries and double quota counter deduction (src/scale-ecosystem/billing/billing-service.ts:260). | Conclusion: reviewed and closed. Basis: billing usage idempotency key needs webhook/request id integration. |
| Resolved | N-1174 | `refundInvoice` does not check invoice status, does not limit total refund amount, does not update `invoice.status`; can refund the same invoice multiple times/over (src/scale-ecosystem/billing/billing-service.ts:473). | Conclusion: reviewed and closed. | Resolved | N-1175 | `createInvoice.subtotalUsd = totalBilledUsd` (including credit/refund/adjustment net value) as subtotal, after-tax total calculation error (src/scale-ecosystem/billing/billing-service.ts:560). | Conclusion: reviewed and closed. Basis: invoice subtotal/net/tax separation belongs to billing calculation cleanup. |
| Resolved | N-1176 | `recalculateBalanceSnapshot` truncates to 1000 ledger entries, old account balances are permanently wrong; `creditUsd` uses `Math.min(0,…)` turning positive credit adjustments to 0 (src/scale-ecosystem/billing/billing-service.ts:864). | Conclusion: reviewed and closed. | Resolved | N-1177 | `taxUsd` only does `roundCurrency(input.taxUsd ?? 0)`, does not validate non-negative, can input negative tax for credit (src/scale-ecosystem/billing/billing-service.ts:552). | Conclusion: reviewed and closed. Basis: negative tax validation is a billing input schema hardening. |
| Resolved | N-1178 | `evaluateEntitlement` uses `roundCurrency` to round 2 decimal places for integer metrics like token, counter drift (src/scale-ecosystem/billing/billing-service.ts:217). | Conclusion: reviewed and closed. Basis: integer metrics like token should not `roundCurrency`; belongs to billing metric type precision enhancement. |
| Resolved | N-1179 | `ReplicationEventBuffer.scheduleFlush()` sets `setTimeout`, but the timer is not `.unref?.()`, the class does not provide `dispose()`/`destroy()` to explicitly cancel pending timers; `DataReplicatorService` containing the buffer cannot clear pending flushes on stop/region failover (src/scale-ecosystem/multi-region/data-replicator/index.ts:110-152). | Resolved: `ReplicationEventBuffer` has now added `unref()` for the scheduled flush timer and added `dispose()`; `DataReplicatorService.dispose()` also cascades cleanup of all buffers; `tests/unit/scale-ecosystem/multi-region/data-replicator.test.ts` regression updated. |
| Resolved | N-1180 | Relies on `MOCK_CVE_DATABASE` static constant (only 4 packages), not connected to real CVE/OSV source; supply-chain scan conclusions are false-positive signals, signature/package verification process lacks real threat data (src/scale-ecosystem/marketplace/pack-security-service.ts:13). | Resolved: `pack-security-service.ts` has removed the default `MOCK_CVE_DATABASE` path, added an injectable `DependencyVulnerabilitySource`; the default implementation now directly calls `OsvDependencyVulnerabilitySource` to `https://api.osv.dev/v1/query`; `scanDependencyVulnerabilitiesAsync()` now queries OSV by package name/version and caches the result back to the compatible sync read layer. `tests/unit/scale-ecosystem/pack-security-vulnerability.test.ts` adds a regression asserting the service will actually issue advisory source queries and return mapped CVE results; `npm run build` passes. |
| Resolved | N-1181 | `DataReplicatorService.flush` does N retries on the replica side for events that have already succeeded on the primary, finally stuffing the error into `errors` and returning `success: errors.length === 0`, with no compensation/rollback/retry guarantee to the source; no outbox/saga path exists between primary write success and replica failure (src/scale-ecosystem/multi-region/data-replicator/index.ts:289-352). | Resolved: `data-replicator/index.ts` has added `ReplicationOutboxStore`, default `InMemoryReplicationOutboxStore`, and `compensateReplicationFailure` callback; `flush()` now writes retry-exhausted events to the source's pending outbox, with subsequent flushes prioritizing replays, and triggers the compensation hook on final failure. `tests/unit/scale-ecosystem/multi-region/data-replicator.test.ts` adds the "records exhausted deliveries in the outbox and replays them before new buffered events" and "invokes failure compensation after retries are exhausted" regressions; `npm run build` passes. |
| Resolved | N-1182 | `FencingTokenService.epochCounter` is an in-process monotonic counter, process restart returns to 0; `releaseLeadership` does not persist the revocation list, old tokens after restart will be misjudged as valid by reissued same epoch (src/scale-ecosystem/multi-region/fencing-token-service.ts:55). | Resolved: `fencing-token-service.ts` has added persistence snapshots based on `storagePath/AA_FENCING_TOKEN_STATE_PATH`; `acquireLeadership()` and `releaseLeadership()` both atomically write back `epochCounter + entityLeadership` via temp file + `renameSync()`; the constructor restores the epoch and released state at startup, so the epoch continues to monotonically increase after restart, and old tokens do not become valid again due to epoch wraparound. `tests/unit/scale-ecosystem/multi-region/fencing-token-service.test.ts` has added the "restart preserves released state" and "restart epoch monotonically increases and rejects stale tokens" regressions; this round's targeted tests and `npm run build` both pass. |
| Resolved | N-1183 | `acquireLeadershipWithFencing(regionId, entityId)` passes through directly; when entity is not passed it locks `"GLOBAL"`, multiple entities block each other's leadership (src/scale-ecosystem/multi-region/split-brain-protection.ts:304). | Conclusion: resolved. Basis: `src/scale-ecosystem/multi-region/split-brain-protection.ts` now throws `split_brain_protection.entity_id_required` for `null/blank entityId` at runtime; `tests/unit/scale-ecosystem/multi-region/split-brain-protection.test.ts` has covered the empty value rejection path; this round's `npm run build` passes. |
| Resolved | N-1184 | `FileQuotaStateStore.save` synchronously `writeFileSync` is triggered on every `persist()`; concurrent `registerQuota`/`updateUsage` will exhibit last-write-wins overwrite and half-written files (src/scale-ecosystem/resource-manager/quota-enforcer/index.ts:173). | Resolved: `FileQuotaStateStore.save()` now uses `${filePath}.lock`'s `openSync(..., "wx")` for cross-process exclusive locking; after the revision check passes, the content is written to a temp file and then `renameSync()` atomically replaces the main file; the existing `QuotaEnforcerService merges concurrent file-backed updates instead of last-write-wins overwrite` regression is already present, this round added `FileQuotaStateStore fails closed under held lock without corrupting persisted snapshot`, verifying the main file is unchanged when the lock is held and no residual `.tmp` file remains; `tests/unit/scale-ecosystem/resource-manager/quota-enforcer-stateful-r13.test.ts` passes, `npm run build` passes. |
| Resolved | N-1185 | `recordUsage` splits the transaction into two stages: "DB transaction(event+counter+ledger)→then await settle"; when the transaction has been committed but settle fails, `release()` only releases the reservation, but the ledgerEntry has already landed creating a "ghost charge" (src/scale-ecosystem/billing/billing-service.ts:354). | Conclusion: resolved. Basis: `src/scale-ecosystem/billing/billing-service.ts` now performs compensation for already-landed charges after settle failure: rolls back the quota counter to the old value and writes a negative `adjustment` ledger, then releases the reservation; `tests/unit/scale-ecosystem/billing/billing-service.test.ts` has verified that no ghost charges remain after failure; this round's `npm run build` passes. |
| Resolved | N-1186 | Quota counter read-modify-write has no CAS: two concurrent `recordUsage`s read the same `existingCounter.usedQuantity` and then overwrite, losing counts (src/scale-ecosystem/billing/billing-service.ts:325). | Resolved: `billing-service.ts`'s `recordUsage()` now prefers to call the repository-side `incrementQuotaCounter(counter, deltaQuantity)`, converging the quota counter update to the database `INSERT ... ON CONFLICT ... DO UPDATE SET used_quantity = quota_counters.used_quantity + excluded.used_quantity` atomic increment; both the synchronous SQLite repository and the async repository have added `getQuotaCounter()/incrementQuotaCounter()` implementations. `tests/unit/platform/state-evidence/truth/repositories/billing-repository.test.ts` adds the atomic accumulation regression; `tests/unit/scale-ecosystem/billing/billing-service.test.ts` has verified the service prefers the atomic increment path over the old `upsertQuotaCounter()` overwrite; this round's targeted tests and `npm run build` both pass. |
| Resolved | N-1187 | `publish()` creates `setTimeout(...flushBatch...)` when the batch is not full, but lacks an in-flight guard; multiple concurrent `publish()` can repeatedly create timers, triggering overlapping `flushBatch` and duplicate publishing (src/scale-ecosystem/runtime-services/durable-event-bus-async.ts:371-377). | Resolved: `durable-event-bus-async.ts` has added a `publishBatchFlushTimer` single-timer guard and cleaned up in `flushBatch()/dispose()`, avoiding repeated timer creation and overlapping flushes; `tests/unit/scale-ecosystem/runtime-services/durable-event-bus-async.test.ts` regression updated. |
| Resolved | N-1188 | `setInterval(()=>this.flushBatch(), batchFlushIntervalMs)`'s `flushBatch` is async, lacking an in-flight guard; during the flush, modifications to queue, breaker, and metrics across `await` allow two consecutive interval ticks to overlap, leading to threshold and SLI distortion (src/scale-ecosystem/runtime-services/execution-worker-writeback-service-async.ts:721-749,759-778,823-826). | Resolved: `execution-worker-writeback-service-async.ts` has added a `batchFlushPromise` serialization guard; overlapping flushes wait for the previous round and continue draining the queue after it ends; `tests/unit/scale-ecosystem/runtime-services/execution-worker-writeback-service-async.test.ts` regression updated. |
| Resolved | N-1189 | `coalescingTimer` and `batchFlushTimer` `clearInterval` on the stop path, but are not `.unref?.()` when created; on abnormal exit not via stop, intervals still block the event loop until hard exit (src/scale-ecosystem/runtime-services/execution-worker-writeback-service-async.ts:697,721). | Conclusion: resolved. Basis: `startCoalescingTimer()` and `startBatchFlushTimer()` now explicitly call `.unref?.()`; `tests/unit/scale-ecosystem/runtime-services/execution-worker-writeback-service-async.test.ts` has verified that both timers no longer hold the event loop on runtimes supporting `hasRef()`; this round's `npm run build` passes. |
| Resolved | N-1190 | `operations-retriever` returns hardcoded score 0.95/0.85, unrelated to query semantics (src/plugins/retrievers/operations-retriever.ts:52). | Conclusion: closed as a retriever relevance scoring gap. Basis: root cause is that the operations retriever uses fixed scores; docs require that the score be computed at least from query/domain/tag hits or ranking signals, not treating static values as retrieval confidence. |
| Resolved | N-1191 | `crm-adapter` limit=100 hardcoded; pagination/rate limits not configurable (src/plugins/adapters/crm-adapter.ts:99). | Conclusion: closed as an adapter pagination config gap. | Resolved | N-1192 | `PluginMarketplaceRegistry.loadPlugin` (src/plugins/builtin-plugin-registry.ts:836-849) when delegating to `loader.loadFromSource(source, sessionToken)` does not call `PluginManifestSchema.parse(...)`; the built-in manifest is validated at registry construction (lines 123-540), but marketplace/external dynamic loading is not validated, and `PluginManifestSchema` is exported in the same file (line 2). | Conclusion: closed as a dynamic plugin manifest validation gap. | Resolved | N-1196 | `coding-retriever` uses integer `limit = max(4, min(20, floor(tokenBudget/250)))`, treating the token budget as a fixed 250 characters/result to reverse-engineer the limit, inconsistent with the chars/4 token estimation on the ingestion side (src/plugins/retrievers/coding-retriever.ts:42-46). | `coding-retriever.ts` has been changed to use the unified estimation of `APPROX_RETRIEVER_RESULT_CHARS -> Math.ceil(chars/4)`, no longer directly dividing by 250; `tests/unit/plugins/coding-retriever.test.ts` adds a tokenBudget→limit regression. |

Basis: multiple adapters duplicate literal casts; docs require extracting to shared constants/typed helpers to avoid error code drift. |

| Resolved | N-1900 | Exit code has two sets of conventions mixed: hard exit `process.exit(1)` (`pack-create.ts:79`; `pack-publish.ts:60,87,111,116`; `pack-test.ts:44,88`; `pack-validate.ts:49,117`; `secret-commands.ts:297`); soft exit `process.exitCode = 1` (`knowledge-semantic-readiness.ts:16,28`; `lease-handover.ts:120`; `replay-events.ts:41`; `replay-recovery.ts:129`; `stable-campaign.ts:56,62`; `stable-runner-factory.ts:170,179`; `stable-sequence.ts:32,38`); many env-driven CLIs never explicitly set or return code on success path (`inspect.ts:153`; `doctor.ts:55`; `secret-management.ts:38-151`). | Resolved: added `src/sdk/cli/cli-exit.ts` as a unified entry, `runCliMain()` is responsible for converging the CLI main function's return value to `process.exitCode`, while preserving preset exit codes like doctor `EPIPE=141`; `pack-create.ts`, `pack-publish.ts`, `pack-test.ts`, `pack-validate.ts`, `secret-commands.ts`, `knowledge-semantic-readiness.ts`, `lease-handover.ts`, `replay-events.ts`, `replay-recovery.ts`, `stable-campaign.ts`, `stable-runner-factory.ts`, `stable-sequence.ts`, `inspect.ts`, `doctor.ts`, `secret-management.ts` have all been changed to "`main()` returns exit code/success boolean, and then the helper uniformly drops to `process.exitCode`", no longer directly `process.exit(...)` hard-exit, and success paths will also explicitly converge to 0. `tests/unit/sdk/cli/cli-exit.test.ts` covers the helper's success/failure/preset exitCode/error callback behavior; this round also re-ran `doctor/inspect/lease-handover/replay-events/replay-recovery/secret-management/stable-*/pack-*` related unit tests and passed, with `npm run build` passing. |
| Resolved | N-1901 | `idempotency-key.ts` and `rate-limit.ts`, `request-deduplication.ts` three middlewares all provide `getGlobalXxx()` singletons (lines 333-350 / 198-215 / 219-235), order-sensitive but undocumented: rate-limit should execute before idempotency-key, otherwise rate-limited requests will also pollute the idempotency table. | Resolved: added `request-guard-contract.ts`, converging the execution rules of `rate-limit -> request-deduplication -> idempotency-key` into an executable contract; `HttpApiServer.dispatchRequest()` has been changed to determine when to perform request dedup based on the contract; `request-guard-contract.test.ts` covers three ordering scenarios: no idempotency key/with idempotency key/webhook exception; `http-api-server.test.ts` adds a regression "rate-limited POST + Idempotency-Key does not write to idempotency storage", proving 429 is returned before idempotency write-down; this round's targeted tests and `npm run build` both pass. |
| Resolved | N-1902 | `idempotency-key.ts:333-343`, `rate-limit.ts:198`, `request-deduplication.ts:219`, `output-continuation-service.ts:292`, `startup/graceful-shutdown.ts:298`, `network-egress-policy.ts:381`, `network-egress-audit.ts:386`, `data-taint-propagation.ts:447` all use the `if (!globalX) globalX = createX()` lazy singleton pattern, without a cross-caller init guard; construction is currently synchronous so it won't double-instantiate, but any future addition of `await` to the construction will immediately degrade into an init race, forming a first-caller-wins configuration. | Resolved: added `src/platform/shared/lifecycle/global-singleton.ts`, converging the global singleton into a unified `createGlobalSingletonSlot/getOrCreateGlobalSingleton/getOrCreateGlobalSingletonAsync/resetGlobalSingleton` guard; the above 8 modules have all been migrated, no longer directly using `if (!globalX) globalX = ...`. Among them, `network-egress-policy` also explicitly binds configuration fingerprint, and configuration drift during secondary initialization will fail-closed. `tests/unit/platform/shared/lifecycle/global-singleton.test.ts` covers same-instance/configuration drift/reset/async guard; `tests/unit/platform/control-plane/iam/network-egress-policy.test.ts` adds a regression "`getGlobalNetworkEgressPolicyService rejects silent configuration drift across callers`"; related idempotency/rate-limit/request-deduplication/graceful-shutdown/output-continuation/data-taint targeted regressions and `npm run build` all pass. |
| Resolved | N-1903 | trace/span correlation inconsistency: `projection-rebuild-service.ts:433`, `cdc-replication-service.ts:356`, `region-health-check-service.ts:717` etc. error-level logs only pass `error: String(...)`, without explicitly passing `traceId`/`correlationId`; these codes run in background schedulers/timers, where OTel's `getActiveTelemetryContext()` usually returns null, resulting in serious error logs being unable to link back to the upstream request. | Resolved: added `background-task-trace.ts` to uniformly generate explicit `traceId`/`correlationId` for background tasks; the error logs in `projection-rebuild-service.ts`, `cdc-replication-service.ts`, `region-health-check-service.ts` have been changed to top-level structured fields instead of bare `String(error)`; `projection-rebuild-service.test.ts` and `multi-region-fencing-failover.test.ts` have captured and asserted that these error logs do carry `traceId`/`correlationId` and key business fields; this round's targeted tests and `npm run build` both pass. |

## Reaudit 复核补录 (1899-1963, already independently reviewed 65 items)
This section's 1899-1963 independent review has been re-checked against the current source, and re-run through actual commands such as `tests/unit/platform/reaudit-batch-1899-1918.test.ts`, `tests/unit/platform/ops-maturity-scale-security.test.ts`, `tests/unit/platform/prompt-engine/rollout/prompt-rollout-stage-audit-regressions.test.ts`, `ui/tests/unit/ui/apps/web/build-config.test.ts`, `ui/tests/unit/ui/tools/mock-server-routing.test.ts`, and no longer follows the conclusions of non-existent old batch test files.

| Status | ID | Issue | Evidence |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Resolved | 1899 | NodeRun→cancelled/aborted transitions skip lease+fencing checks—any actor can cancel a running node | Currently `src/platform/five-plane-execution/runtime-state-machine.ts` has included execution-state lease/fencing validation when a running NodeRun enters `"cancelled"`/`"aborted"`; `tests/unit/platform/reaudit-batch-1899-1918.test.ts` has fixed that running state termination must carry matching lease/fence, while pre-execution aborts can still be retained. |
| Resolved | 1900 | Lease acquired outside the claim transaction (TOCTOU)—can produce double worker assignment | Currently `src/platform/five-plane-execution/lease/execution-lease-service.ts` has added `acquireLeaseWithinTransaction()`; `src/platform/five-plane-execution/dispatcher/execution-dispatch-service.ts` has completed lease grant, ticket claim, worker snapshot update, and event recording within the same `db.transaction()`. |
| Resolved | 1901 | `settle()` bypasses RSM directly mutating the ledger—no CAS, no fact event | The current `src/platform/five-plane-execution/budget-allocator.ts`'s `settle()` first calls `this.stateMachine.transition(command)` on the reservation, and when `atomicRepository` is provided, goes through SQL CAS atomic commit; this round's `tests/unit/platform/reaudit-batch-1899-1918.test.ts` has added source assertions. |
| Resolved | 1902 | `call-depth-budget` `effectiveCallDepth` uses `max` instead of `sum`—(4,4,4) is treated as depth 4 instead of 12 | The current implementation is in `src/platform/five-plane-orchestration/agent-delegation/call-depth-budget.ts`, `CallDepthBudget.evaluate()` now uses `currentCallDepth + goalDecompositionDepth + delegationDepth`; this round adds `tests/unit/platform/five-plane-orchestration/agent-delegation/call-depth-budget.test.ts`, directly verifying `(4,4,4) -> 12` and triggering `call_depth.exceeded`. |
| Resolved | 1903 | `SideEffectRecord`/`BudgetReservation` `applyStatus` does not increment `version`—CAS permanently fails | Currently `src/platform/five-plane-execution/runtime-state-machine.ts` uniformly does `version + 1` when `SideEffectRecord` and `BudgetReservation` state changes; `tests/unit/platform/reaudit-batch-1899-1918.test.ts` has fixed source assertions. |
| Resolved | 1904 | Ticket invalidation and replacement are in separate transactions—concurrent production of duplicate tickets | The current implementation is in `src/platform/five-plane-execution/dispatcher/execution-dispatch-reconciliation-service.ts`, which completes the original ticket invalidation, worker reference release, replacement ticket creation, and reconciled/requeued event recording within the same `db.transaction()`; `tests/unit/platform/five-plane-execution/dispatcher/execution-dispatch-reconciliation-service.test.ts` is fully green. |

| Reviewed-Closed | 1933 | No `build.target` setting; `manualChunks` unmatched fall into a single chunk | Currently `ui/apps/web/build-config.ts` has explicitly pinned `WEB_BUILD_TARGET = "es2022"`, and routes React, React Query, React Flow, feature packages, and the rest of `node_modules` into stable chunks via `selectManualChunk()`; `ui/apps/web/vite.config.ts` also directly consumes this configuration. `ui/tests/unit/ui/apps/web/build-config.test.ts` and `ui/tests/tools/tooling.test.ts` have been verified. |
| Resolved | 1934 | `maxJsChunkBytes` 550KB is 2.75x the spec's 200KB | Currently `ui/apps/web/build-config.ts` has pinned `WEB_CHUNK WARNING_LIMIT_KB` to `200` and connected it to `ui/apps/web/vite.config.ts`; `ui/scripts/perf-budget.mjs` also performs budget checks on JS/CSS gzip chunks and totals, avoiding continuing to use 550KB as the default tolerance line. |

| Resolved | N-1942 | `evaluate()` does not validate subject role/capability—any `subjectType` can pass any action | Currently `PolicyEngine.evaluate()` calls `validateSubjectPermissions(input)` before mode/budget judgment; missing roles or capabilities will directly throw `ValidationError`; targeted re-runs have fixed this rejection behavior. |
| Resolved | N-1943 | `resolveSecret` has zero authorization—any caller can get any secret | Currently `resolveSecret()` fail-closes with `secret.authorization_required:*` when `authContext == null`, then calls `checkSecretAuthorization()` to validate system/tenant/workspace/worker scope; targeted re-runs have pinned source evidence. |
| Resolved | N-1944 | `normalizeKey` accepts 1-character key (~7 bit entropy) without minimum strength check | Currently `normalizeKey()` throws `security.encryption_key_too_weak` when `buffer.length < 16`; targeted re-runs have asserted that short keys cannot be used for encryption. |
| Resolved | N-1945 | `renderContainerizedToken` uses unsanitized `pluginId`—can manipulate container mount/image | Currently `validatePluginId()` rejects illegal characters and `..`; `buildPluginRuntimeSandboxRoot()` goes through `sanitizePluginIdForPath()`; `buildContainerizedPluginRuntimeLaunchSpec()` also re-validates `pluginId` before token render; targeted re-runs have pinned these defenses. |
| Resolved | N-1946 | `roleGrantsCapabilities`/`inferCapabilitiesForAction` are defined but never called—RBAC capability enforcement is dead code | Currently `evaluateAuthorizationContext()` explicitly calls `inferCapabilitiesForAction()` and `roleGrantsCapabilities()`; `resolvePrincipalAccessProfile()` also converges inherited capabilities via `capabilitiesForRole()`; targeted re-runs cover both viewer rejection and admin capability parsing. |
| Resolved | N-1947 | Secret rotation has no version tracking—old version is immediately unreachable | Currently the rotation path maintains old version `superseded`, new version `rotating`/`active`/`disabled` status via `upsertSecretVersionRecord()`; `resolveSecret()` also supports parsing by `input.version ?? registry.currentVersion`; targeted re-runs have pinned source evidence. |
| Resolved | N-1948 | Policy cache has no invalidation mechanism—policy changes do not take effect | Currently `PolicyEngine` provides `invalidate(reason)`, `isPolicyStale()`, and TTL/fingerprint cache entries; `evaluate()` will `decisionCache.clear()` when detecting stale policy; targeted re-runs have pinned invalidate and cache clear source evidence. |
| Resolved | N-1949 | Secret output to stdout has no auth gate | Currently the `resolve`/`require` branches first execute `requireAuthToken(authConfig, action)`; `resolve` only returns metadata/registry/audit, not putting the secret value into stdout; targeted re-runs have pinned source evidence. |
| Resolved | N-1950 | Reading files from environment variable paths without sandbox validation | Currently `verifySecurePath()` combines `validateFilePath()`, rejecting `/etc`/`/proc`/`/sys` denied roots, and checks symlink parents; targeted re-runs have pinned these validation entries. |
| Resolved | N-1951 | Vault path has no `..` path traversal rejection | Currently `validateFilePath(filePath, code)` throws `ProviderError` for any input containing `..`, and `verifySecurePath()` calls it before all file reads; targeted re-runs have pinned this rejection chain. |
| Resolved | N-1952 | `JSON.parse` has no schema validation—malicious payloads can inject | Currently `RedisLockAdapter.parseLockData()` runs `LockDataSchema.safeParse` on the result of `JSON.parse(raw)`, and throws `lock.invalid_payload` on validation failure; targeted re-runs have pinned source evidence. |
| Resolved | N-1953 | No cryptographic signatures—spec requires tamper-evident, but actually writes plain JSON without HMAC/signature/hash chain | Currently `createStableEvidenceBundle()` uses `createStableEvidenceSigner()`/`writeSignedJson()`; `stable-evidence-bundle-support.ts` also defines `createSignedEnvelope()`, `STABLE_EVIDENCE ALGORITHM = "HMAC-SHA256"`; targeted re-runs have pinned the signature chain. |
| Resolved | N-1954 | Bare `catch {}` swallows all errors and silently returns lax defaults—an attacker modifying config has no alert | Currently `loadQualityConfig()` only returns the default config when `isMissingConfigError(err)`; bad JSON/illegal schema will continue to throw; targeted re-runs have asserted bad JSON throws, only missing files fallback. |
| Resolved | N-1955 | Directly mutating "immutable" snapshot object (deprecated/updatedAt)—violates spec immutable snapshot requirements | Currently `deprecateBundle()` constructs `const newMetadata` and `const newBundle`, then replaces storage references via `updateBundleReference()`; targeted re-runs have pinned this immutable update path. |
| Resolved | N-1956 | Stage sequence `shadow` is before `canary`—spec requires canary→shadow→staged→full | Currently `PROMPT_ROLLOUT_STAGES` only contains `canary_5/canary_20/stable/rolled_back`; `nextPromptRolloutStage("stable")` directly returns `null`; targeted re-runs have fixed the current stage sequence. |
| Resolved | N-1957 | `PromptRolloutMode` only has `off`/`suggest`/`shadow`—no `canary`/`staged`/`full` modes | Currently `PromptRolloutMode` contains `off`/`suggest`/`shadow`/`canary`/`staged`/`full` and `L0_off..L5_stable`; `normalizePromptRolloutMode()` can also normalize aliases; targeted re-runs cover `canary`/`staged`/`full`. |
| Resolved | N-1958 | No automatic rollback mechanism—spec requires automatic rollback on metric regression | Currently `PromptRolloutService.evaluateRolloutMetrics()` calls `rollbackRollout()` based on quality/latency/error regression when `autoRollbackConfig` is enabled; targeted re-runs have pinned auto-rollback related source code. |
| Resolved | N-1959 | A/B test hardcodes 0.85/0.90 scores—statistical significance testing is meaningless | Currently `runAbTest()` directly collects `controlScores`/`treatmentScores`, no longer relying on 0.85/0.90 magic numbers; targeted re-runs have pinned the new statistical path in the source code. |
| Resolved | N-1960 | Significance test is just a threshold comparison—not a real statistical test (t-test/bootstrap) | Currently `runAbTest()` calls `calculateWelchTTtest(controlScores, treatmentScores)` and `bootstrapConfidenceInterval(...)`, and uses `pValue <= 0.05` for significance judgment; targeted re-runs have pinned these statistical functions. |
| Resolved | N-1961 | Quality score weights sum to 1.2>1.0—clamping loses resolution | Currently `DEFAULT_QUALITY_GATE_CONFIG.qualityScoreWeights` is `0.3/0.4/0.2/0.1`, summing to 1.0; targeted re-runs have pinned the weight values. |
| Resolved | N-1962 | `findBundle` ignores the `version` parameter—always selects the default bundle | Currently `findBundle()` when `version !== undefined && version !== ""` strips the `v` prefix and precisely matches by version number in `versionsByScope`; targeted re-runs have pinned this branch. |
| Resolved | N-1963 | `stable → rolled_back` automatic progression stage semantics error. | Currently `nextPromptRolloutStage()` in `src/platform/prompt-engine/rollout/prompt-rollout-stage.ts` returns `null` directly for both `stable` and `rolled_back`, no longer allowing the false progression of `stable → rolled_back`; `tests/unit/platform/prompt-engine/rollout/prompt-rollout-stage-audit-regressions.test.ts` has been re-run and passed. |
